“ā toṇṭiccōḻaṉ. ivar tantiyākiya maṇikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ allatu kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ oru nāḷ vēṭṭikkēkiya vaṉattil, oru riṣi kaṉṉikiyākiya nākakaṉṉikiyi ātoṇṭip pūmāli cātti, kāntarva vivākañ ceytu koṇṭataṉāl piṟantavar. ivar tanti taṉatu irācciyattiṉ teṉpākatti makariṣiyāṉa kumāraṉukkuk koṭuttum vaṭapākatti ātoṇṭarukkuk koṭuttum āḷac ceytārām. ivar āṇṭa pākattiṟkut toṇṭi maṇṭala meṉṟu peyarākiyatu. ivar tamatu nāṭṭil vēḷāḷarik kuṭiyēṟṟic ciṟappittapaṭiyāl avarkaḷ toṇṭi maṇṭala vēḷāḷar eṉṉum peyar peṟṟārkaḷ.” (takkaṇa intiya carittiram).
at toṇṭimaṇṭalattiṉ elliyāvaṉa:-
“mēṟkup pavaḷamali vēṅkaṭanēr vaṭakkā
mārkku muvari yaṇikiḻakku-pārkkuḷuyar
teṟkup piṉāki tikaḻirupatiṉ kātam
naṟṟoṇṭi nāṭeṉavē nāṭṭu.”
it toṇṭi maṇṭalattiṟkuk kāñcipuram talinakaram. “nākappaṭṭiṉattuc cōḻaṉ pilattuvārattāl nākalōkattē ceṉṟu nākakaṉṉikiyip puṇarnta kālattu avaḷ yāṉ peṟṟa putalvaṉi eṉ ceyvēṉeṉṟapoḻutu toṇṭiyi (toṇṭi-kōvikkoṭi) aṭiyāḷamākak kaṭṭik kaṭalil, viṭa, avaṉ vantu kariyēṟiṉ avaṟku yāṉ aracurimiyi eytuvittu nāṭāṭci koṭuppaṉeṉṟu avaṉ kūṟa, avaḷum putalvaṉi varaviṭat tiri tarutaliṉ tiriyaṉeṉṟu peyar peṟṟa kati kūṟiṉār. (nacciṉārkiṉiyaruri-perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭi)
6. koṅkunāṭu
“vaṭakkuttali maliyām viyāvūr teṟku
kuṭakkuveḷ ḷipporuppuk kuṉṟu-kiḻakku
kaḻittaṇṭali cūḻum kāvirinaṉ ṉāṭām
kuḻittaṇ ṭaliyaḷavuṅ koṅku.”
vaṭakku-talimali (itu micūrukkum cattiyamaṅkalattukku miṭiyi luḷḷatu. cattiyamaṅkalam kōyamuttūriṉ oru pakuti tālukā) teṟku -vikāvūr (itu paḻaṉikku iṉṉoru peyar); mēṟku-veḷḷi yaṅkiri mali. itu maliyāḷattukkum kōyamuttūrukkum iṭi yiluḷḷa maliyiṉ peyar (North Palghat Gap) vaṭakku-kāviri nīrpāyccum kuḻittali . amarāvati kāviric caṅkamam kuḻittalikkuc cila milkaḷ mēṟkiluḷḷatu. itu kaṉṉaṭam cērnta koṅku nāṭu. ātiyil koṅku nāṭu, kōyamuttūrum cēlattiṉ teṟkup pātiyumaṭaṅkiya nāṭākavirun tatu. atu 24 nāṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. kaṉṉaṭariṉ veṟṟikkup piṉ itaṉ elli ikkāla micūr variyum akaṟṟappaṭṭatu.
7. ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷ
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷi yaṉṟik kuṉṟutōṟuṅ kōṭṭi kaḷi vaḷittuk kaṭṭi mūvēntarukkuk kīḻppaṭṭum paṭātum ciṟṟaracu purinta pala aracarkaḷik kuṟittuc ceytikaḷ pala, puṟanāṉūṟṟiṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvaracarkkuriya nāṭukaḷ ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷām.
8. pallavar, cāḷukkiyar
pallavarkaḷ kāñci nakarit talinakaramākak koṇṭu ki.mu.300 mutal aṟunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ variyil āṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kālattil appar campantar ciṟuttoṇṭar mutaliya civa aṭiyārkaḷ viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. “toṇṭimāṉ eṉpataṟkuc cariyāṉa camakkiruta moḻi peyarppē pallavar” eṉac varalāṟṟukkārar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. pallavarkaḷi veṉṟu kāñciyik kippaṟṟic cilakālam cāḷukkiyar aracu ceytārkaḷ. maṇipallavam eṉappaṭṭa yāḻppāṇattup paṇṭi nākaaraca parampariyiluḷḷōrē piṟkālattup pallavareṉappaṭṭaṉa reṉpatu oru cārār karuttu.
4. tamiḻiṉ ciṟappu
“kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō”
eṉṟu āṉṟōr kūṟiya poruḷ ciṟcila moḻikaḷi oppiṭṭu nōkkuvārkkum iṉitu viḷaṅkum. paṇṭik kālantoṭṭu nūl vaḻakkiṉum ulaka vaḻakkiṉum tirutta maṭintu makkaḷaṟiviṉ mutirccikkup peritum utavi ceyvaṉavāyp pōtarum paḻiya moḻikaḷ cilavēyām. ippaḻiya moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻmoḻiyi oḻittu oḻintavaṟṟiṟ perumpālaṉa ulakavaḻakkiṉṟi iṟantoḻintaṉa. tīñcuvi viḷikkum muppaḻattiṉum iṉiya melliya ōci iṉpam vāynta centamiḻ moḻiyō, ettaṉiyō āyiram āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintum taṉ iḷami keṭātu kaḻiperu makiḻcciyōṭu ulavi varutali uṟṟu nōkkuṅkāl, ataṉi vaḻaṅkivanta naṉmakkaḷ evvaḷavu nuṇṇaṟivum, evvaḷavu amitiyāṉa taṉmiyum, evvaḷavu nākarikamum uṭiyavarkaḷāy iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpati aṟikiṉṟōm. paḻiya nāṭkaḷil vēṟu pala moḻikaḷip pēci vanta makkaḷ, nuṇṇaṟivilum aminta kuṇattilum nākarikac ciṟappuṟṟavarkaḷā yillāmiyiṉāl, avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻikaḷ ellām āṇṭukaṭōṟum māṟutalkaḷ pala eyti, ilakkaṇa varampil akappaṭāvāyp payaṉiṉṟik kaḻintaṉa. tamiḻic cūḻa iññāṉṟu naṭipeṟum pala moḻikaḷi ārāyntu pārppavarkku ammoḻikaḷ ōr ilakkaṇa varampillāmal palapaṭac citaṟi oḻuṅkiṉṟik kiṭattal teḷḷitiṟ pulaṉākum.
“tamiḻ kirēkkamoḻiyiṉum nayamāṉa ceyyuḷnaṭiyuṭiyatu
lattīṉ moḻiyiṉum pūraṇamāṉatu” (viṉslō)
“maṉitarāṟ pēcappaṭukiṉṟa mikap polivum tiruttamum cīrumuṭiya moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻum oṉṟu.” (ṭeylar)
tamiḻiṉ ciṟappi uṇarttum ceyyuṭkaḷ
“poruppilē piṟantu teṉṉaṉ pukaḻilē kiṭantu caṅkat
tiruppilē yiruntu viki yēṭṭilē tavaḻnta pēti
neruppilē niṉṟu kaṟṟōr niṉivilē naṭantō rēṉa
maruppilē payiṉṟa pāvi maruṅkilē vaḷarukiṉṟāḷ” (villipāratam)
“kaṭukkaviṉpeṟu kaṇṭaṉun teṉṟici nōkki
aṭukkavantuvan tāṭuvā ṉāṭali ṉiḷippu
viṭukkavārameṉ kāltiru mukattiṭi vīci
maṭukkavuntamiḻ tiruccevi māntavu maṉṟō”
“toṇṭar nātaṉit tūtiṭi viṭuttatu mutali
uṇṭapālaṉi yaḻittatu melumpu peṇṇuruvāk
kaṇṭatum maṟikkataviṉit tiṟantatuṅ kaṉṉit
taṇṭamiḻc collō maṟupulac coṟkaḷō cāṟṟīr” (tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam)
“tamaranīrp puvaṉa muḻutoruṅ kīṉṟāḷ
taṭātakātēvi yeṉ ṟorupēr
tarikkavan tatuvun taṉimuta lorunī
cavuntara māṟaṉā ṉatuvuṅ
kumaravēḷ vaḻuti yukkiraṉeppēr
koṇṭatun taṇṭamiḻ maturaṅ
kūṭṭuṇa veḻunta vēṭkiyā lelilik
koḻitamiḻp perumiyā raṟivār” (maturik kalampakam)
“ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷkaṭiyum-āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatirōṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ” (taṇṭi-uri-mēṟkōḷ)
“vēliyil vīḻtta kallu meṉkuṭam pukutta veṉpuñ
cāliyiṟ koḷuvun tīyun taraṅkanīr viki yāṟuñ
cōliyāṇ paṉiyum vētak katavamun toḻumpu koṇṭa
vāliyān tamiḻppūñ celvi....” (tirukkuṟṟālat talapurāṇam)
“nīrāruṅ kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭantik keḻiloḻukuñ
cīrārum vataṉameṉat tikaḻparata kaṇṭamitil
takkaciṟu piṟinutalum tarittanaṟun tilakamumē
tekkaṇamum atiṟciṟanta tiraviṭa naṟ ṟirunāṭum
attilaka vācaṉipōl aṉittulakum iṉpamuṟa
etticiyum pukaḻ maṇakka irunta perun tamiḻaṇaṅkē.”
(maṉōṉmaṇīyam)
ivvakip pāṭalkaḷ eḻutaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ
tācukkaḷ, āriyar eṉappaṭum makkaḷukkiṭiyē naṭanta yuttaṅ kaḷippaṟṟi vēta pāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tācukkaḷ eṉappaṭṭōr tamiḻar. ivvuṇarcciyiṉ vēkam nīṇṭakālam iruntu vantatu. itaṉāl tamiḻi āriyattilum tāḻntatākak kūṟum kaṭciyoṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil nīṇṭakālamtoṭṭu iruntu varuvatāyiṟṟu. camayakuravar kālattum ivvaki uṇarcciyiruntimiyiṉālēyē iru kaṭciyiṉariyum cantu ceytaṟ poruṭṭut tēvārattil ‘āriyaṉkaṇṭāy tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭāy’ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tamiḻi iḻivupaṭuttum koṭumi nāḷukkunāḷ vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. atil paṭṭu mayaṅkiya makkaḷ tamiḻiṉ uyarviyum ataṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyiyum aṟiyātu ataṉi ikaḻvārāyiṉar. kalviyiṟ periya kampaṉē ‘tēvapāṭiyiṉ ikkati ceytavar’ eṉak kūṟi vaṭamoḻikkup paṇikiṉṟami kāṇka. parañcōti muṉivar, civañāṉa muṉivar, pērā ciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi mutalāṉōr ammakkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmikku iraṅki, avarkaḷ maṉaṅkoṇṭu tamiḻil paṟṟu uṇṭāmāṟu ataṉ perumiyi urittu vantaṉar. immāṟupaṭṭa uṇarcci iṉṟum muṟṟāka avintuviṭavilli. nīṟupūtta neruppuppōl aṭaṅkikkiṭak kiṉṟatu eṉalām.
tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra uriyil (tol-poruḷ 490)
“āriya naṉṟu tamiḻtī teṉavuritta
kāriyattāṟ kālakkōṭ paṭṭāṉic -cīriya
antaṇ potiyil akattiyaṉā rāṇiyāl
centamiḻē tīrkka cuvā”
eṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷa utāraṇac ceyyuḷālum ivvuṇarcci tamiḻnāṭṭil nīṇṭanāḷ uḷḷateṉpatu naṉi viḷaṅkum.
5. eḻuttu
“eṇṇeṉpa ēṉi yeḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum
kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻu muyirkku.” (kuṟaḷ)
eḻuttu, cuvaṭi mutaliya paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷ tamiḻmoḻikku varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷ toṉmiyē uṇṭu eṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. 1iññāṉṟu tamiḻil viḷaṅkum nūlkaḷuḷ tol kāppiyam paḻimiyuṭiyatu. itaṉkālam ki.mu.350kkup piṟpaṭṭataṉ ṟeṉpatu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttu. vētaṅkaḷ viyācarāl nāṉku kūṟukaḷākac ceyyappaṭuvataṉmuṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭateṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. appaṭiyāyiṉ tolkāppiyar kālam aiyāyiramāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉākum. tolkāppiyattil eḻuttatikārameṉa oru pakuti uḷḷatu. ataṉkaṇ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu vaṭivu mutaliyaṉa kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, tolkāppiyarukkup paṉṉeṭuṅ kālattukku muṉ tamiḻil eḻuttukkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṟṟikku āyira varuṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum eḻuttukkaḷip pōṉṟaṉavalla.ki.pi. patiṉālām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil avveḻuttukkaḷ tiripaṭintu vantaṉavākat terikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi “uruvu tirintu uyirttalāvatu mēluṅ kīḻum vilaṅku peṟṟum kōṭu peṟṟum puḷḷi peṟṟum puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟum uyirttalām. ki-kī mutaliyaṉa mēl vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ku-kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻ vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke,kē mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. kā,ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyi ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyi vaḷittu eḻutiṉār. ko, kō,ṅeh,ṅō mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟaṉa eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyāl aṟiyalākum. muṟkālattut tolkāppiyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭatu vaṭṭeḻuttilēyām. paḻiya vaṭ ṭeḻuttu kōleḻuttu eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ maliyāḷattil mikac camīpa kālattil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷatu. aṅku itu ki.pi.17ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyi lum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. maliyāḷattil māppiḷimār kōleḻutti iṉṟikkum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar eṉpa.1
tiruvaṉantapurattiluḷḷa nūtaṉacāliyil cēkarittu vikkappaṭ ṭirukkum cila cācaṉaṅkaḷāl tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻuttu ki.pi.18ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti variyum vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṟatu.
“akkālattilē vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ ikkālam vaḻaṅkum eḻuttukkaḷip pōṉṟaṉavalla. maturikku vaṭakiḻakkē 9-milukkap pāl iraṇṭu mil toṭarcciyuḷḷa āṉimali yeṉṉum oru malittoṭaruḷḷatu. ammaliyiṉ mēṟkuppakkattil oru kukiyiruk kiṉṟatu. akkukiyiṉ cuvaril paḻiya tamiḻeḻuttu veṭṭappaṭṭa cācaṉamoṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itutāṉ vaṭṭeḻuttu allatu paḻiya tamiḻeḻuttu. eḻuttukkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa vaṭiviliruntu avikku vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
“ic cācaṉattiṉ kālam ki.pi. 770. kiṟittuvukkuc cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ teṉṉintiyāvil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttu ituvēyākum. kiṟittuvukkuppiṉ eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭṭeḻuttuṭaṉ vēṟu vakiyāṉa eḻuttukkaḷiyuṅ kalantu eḻutappaṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷuḷ vaṭṭeḻuttiṉ iṭattip putiya eḻuttu eṭuttuk koṇṭatu. āriyaruṭiya camayamum cāttiraṅ kaḷum teṉṉāṭṭil paravat toṭaṅkiya ētuviṉāl camakkirutac coṟkaḷ pala tamiḻiṟ kalakka nērntatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. ippōtiya vaṭivattit tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ patiṉālām nūṟṟāṇṭil aṭintaṉa.” (Dr. Chandler)
ippōtu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa kiranta eḻuttukkaḷ vaṭṭeḻutti ṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiṉavēyām.
pōrmuṉiyil iṟanta vīraṉukkuk kalnaṭṭu atil avaṉuṭiya peyarum pīṭum eḻutuvatu paḻantamiḻar vaḻakku. itaṉi
“nallamar kaṭanta nāṇuṭi maṟavar
peyarum pīṭum eḻuti atartōṟum
pīli cūṭṭiya piṟaṅkunili naṭukal” (akam)
“paṭṭōr peyaru māṟṟalu meḻuti
naṭṭa kallu mūtūr nattamum” (tiruvārūr mummaṇikkōvi)
eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟālaṟika. iv vīrakkal niṟuttum vaḻakkam paḻantamiḻarkaḷ eḻutteḻutum muṟiyiṉi aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉpati valiyuṟuttu kiṉṟatu.
tamiḻmoḻiyil neṭuṅkaṇakku mikavum aḻaku peṟa amin tuḷḷatu. orē eḻuttukkup pala oliyilli. ōrolikkup pala eḻuttukkaḷ vēṇṭā; kūṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ kiṭiyā. “mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttu muṟi kāṭṭi” eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttiram itaṉi valiyuṟuttum. ellā eḻuttukkaḷum moḻikku mutalil vārā. ellā eḻuttukkaḷum moḻikkiṭiyil mayaṅkā. muṉ ettaṉi eḻuttukkaḷiruntaṉavō attaṉiyē iṉṟuvariyumuḷḷaṉa.
āriya moḻikkuriya neṭuṅkaṇakku tamiḻar muṟiyip pārttuc ceyyappaṭṭateṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar tuṇipu. “tāṅkaḷ cellumiṭaṅ kaḷukkut takkapaṭi putiya lipikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷum iyalpuṭi āriyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kēṟṟapaṭi tamiḻlipiyi oṭṭik ‘kirantam’ eṉṉum putiyatōr lipi vakuttaṉar.” (tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ varalāṟu)
tamiḻmoḻiyiluḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatumē kuṟipāṭil lāta uṟuppukkaḷ aminta makkaḷ, kuṟipāṭillāta muyaṟciyāl vāyāṟ piṟappikkum ōcikaḷākum. camakkiruta moḻikkaṭ kāṇappaṭum tamiḻi lillāta cila eḻuttukkaḷ kuṟipāṭuṭiya muyaṟciyāṟ piṟappikkappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷām. tamiḻukkuriya āyta eḻuttiṉ utaviyikkoṇṭu emmoḻiyiluḷḷa evvaki eḻuttōcikaḷiyum piṟappittal eḷitil amiyum.
immuṟiyiṉi viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉōr āciriyar maṟimali yaṭikaḷ, tiruvāḷar pā.vē. māṇikka nāyakkar mutaliyōrāvar.
“akara mutal oḷakāra iṟuvāyākak kiṭanta paṉṉīreḻuttuk kaḷumē uyireḻuttukkaḷ eṉappaṭuvataṟkum, ivi tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ oṉṟāka aṭivupaṭuttu niṟuttappaṭṭa muṟiyē muṟiyeṉappaṭu taṟkum urimiyuṭiyaṉavām. iḵtu ivvāṟākavum, vaṭanūlār tamiḻiṉ neṭuṅkaṇakkip pārttu uyireḻuttu meyyeḻuttukkaḷi aṭivu paṭuttikkoṇṭa aḷavil amiyātu, tamiḻiṉum tamakku uyireḻut tukkaḷ mikutiyāy uṇṭeṉṟu kāṭṭuvataṟkup pukuntu poruntāp pēravāvāl uyireḻuttukkaḷallāta ru rū lū, am a: eṉṉum uyireḻuttukkaḷiyum avaṟṟōṭu kalantu iḻukkiṉār. ivvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷiṟ kalanta kuṟṟiyalukara ūkāraṅkaḷiyum akaraṅkaḷiyum nīkkiṉāl eñci niṟpaṉa m r lḵ eṉṉum meyyeḻuttukkaḷum āytamumēyākaliṉ avar uyireḻuttukkaḷip paṉṉiraṇṭiṉ mēlākap paṭittiṭṭuk koṇṭatu veṟum pōliyā meṉka.”
“patiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttukkaḷ maṭṭumē cevviyākap poruntappaṭṭa uṟuppukaḷuṭiya makkaḷ kuṟipāṭillāta muyaṟciyāl tam vāyāṟ piṟappippaṉavākum. vintuviṉiṉṟum mēleḻunta vintu kāraṇa voliyit taṉṉiliyil nērē iyaṅkaviṭātu makkaḷ tamatu niṟinta muyaṟciyāl tali miṭaṟu neñcu eṉṉum mūviṭaṅkaḷilum ataṉi niṟuttip, pal, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam eṉṉum aintuṟuppukaḷōṭu ataṉip pala vēṟupaṭat tirittup pala olikaḷāka veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvintu kāraṇavoli kuṟipāṭillāta uṟuppukaḷālum kuṟipā ṭillāta muyaṟcikaḷālum iyakkak kūṭumvari ippatiṉeṭṭeḻuttiṉ mēṟpaṭṭa olikaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkuc ciṟitumiṭamēyilli, maṟṟu ivvā ṟaṉṟik kuṟipāṭuṭiya uṟuppukkaḷum kuṟipāṭuṭiya muyaṟcikaḷu miruntāl vintukāraṇa voli cevvaṉē iyaṅkappaṭāmaṟ palavāṟāy iyaṅki aḷavuk kaṭaṅkāp palatiṟa volikaḷiyellām tōṟṟu vikkum.
“tamiḻil illāmal vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkil maṭṭum kāṇappaṭum cila oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ attuṇiyum tamiḻiṉkaṇ niṟinta muyaṟciyāṟ piṟappikkum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ cilavaṟṟik kuṟinta muyaṟciyāl āytavoliyit taṭippaṭātu cellaviṭuttup piṟappikkat tōṟṟuvaṉavē yākumātalāl, uṇmiyāka nōkkuṅkāl avi taṉittaṉi meyyeḻuttukkaḷ ākamāṭṭā veṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum.” tamiḻil oli eḻuttukkaḷ- āciriyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ.
āriyamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷum piṟavum am moḻikkiṉamākiya ilattiṉ, kirīk, epirēyam mutaliya moḻikaḷil illāmiyāl intiyāvi aṭinta āriyar aṅkuṟinta tolkuṭi kaḷākiya tamiḻariṉ eḻuttu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyatuṭaṉ lipikaḷiyum ākkikkoṇṭārkaḷeṉpatu. . . . . veḷḷiṭi vilaṅkal.
1“pērāciriyar irāpcaṉ, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ mikavum tiruttam peṟṟiruntaṉaveṉṟum, ākavē camakkirutattil tirāviṭak kalappiruttal āccariyappaṭattakkataṉṟu eṉṟum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ilattiṉ kirēkku mutaliya ēṉiya āriya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭātu camakkirutattil māttiram kāṇappaṭum ciṟappukkaḷ āriya makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu peṟṟamiyāl uṇṭāyiṉaveṉṟu ilakuvil koḷḷa lākum.” (tirāviṭa intiyā, pak.75)
“tamiḻc coṟkaḷum vaṭacoṟkaḷ pōlat tātukkaḷi yuṭiyaṉavām. tātuveṉiṉum pakuti yeṉiṉum oṉṟē. tamiḻccoṟ pakutikaḷ ellāc coṟkaḷukkum teṟṟeṉak kāṇpataritu. atupaṟṟiyē tolkāppiyarum ‘moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam viḻippattōṉṟā’ eṉṟār. āyiṉum nuṇṇiyatāka ārāyumiṭattup pulappaṭāmaṟ pōkātu. cila coṟkaḷ viṉiyaṭiyākavum cila coṟkaḷ uriyaṭiyākavum cila coṟkaḷ iṭiyaṭiyākavum oru cila peyaraṭiyākavum piṟantaṉa.
“akattiyar kālattilē vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ pala piṟkālattilē vaḻakkiṟantaṉa. avar kālattilē vaḻaṅkāta coṟkaḷ piṟkālattil vaḻaṅkuvaṉavāyiṉa. avar kālattilum tolkāppiyar kālattilum cakara akaratti mutalākavuṭiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkavilli. caṭṭi caṭṭuvam caṭṭi cantiraṉ cañcalam calli cali mutaliyaṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷalla.” (teṉmoḻi varalāṟu.)
“peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉṉum nālvakic coṟkaḷil iṭiccoṟkaḷum uriccoṟkaḷum muṉṉoru kālattil viṉikaḷā yiruntaṉa. avikaḷaṉittum kālakkiramattil attaṉmiyiḻantu ippōtuḷḷa iṭiyurip paṇpukaḷ aṭivaṉavāyiṉa.” (tamiḻmoḻi yiṉ varalāṟu)
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ mutaṟ paruvattē uḷḷa coṟkaḷ perumpālum ōraciyiṉa veṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam tamiḻukkuḷḷa coṟkaḷ peruntoki yiṉa valla veṉṟum, ovvōr collaṭiyākavum paṟpala coṟkaḷ tōṉṟip palkiṉa veṉṟum col ārāycci valla ñāṉappirakāca ravarkaḷ tamatu ‘tamiḻ moḻi amippuṟṟa varalāṟu’ eṉṉum nūlil viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
“katana pamaveṉu māvin teḻuttum
ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellu mārmutalē”
“cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai oḷa veṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷāl akkālattuc ca, cu mutal moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkavilliyeṉat terikiṟatu. “cakarakkiḷavi”eṉṉum cūttirattiṉiraṇṭāmaṭi, “avi oḷa veṉu moṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē” eṉṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, atu evvāṟō “a ai oḷaveṉu mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē” eṉṟu tiruttappaṭṭateṉṟum tiruvāḷar aṭik kalampiḷḷi avarkaḷ meykaṇṭāṉ pattirikiyil veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷār. itu ārāyccikkuriyatu. akarātikaḷil iraṇṭāyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa cakara mutaṉ moḻikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“yāḻppāṇattilē eḻutat toṭaṅkic ceṉṉiyil pūrtti yākkappaṭṭa viṉslō (Winslow) akarāti mikac ciṟantatākum. itu aṟupattēḻu varuṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. avvakarātiyil 67,000 coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 1913 ām āṇṭil putiya tamiḻp pērakarāti maturiyil toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. atu ippōtu ceṉṉiyil palkalik kaḻakattāriṉ mēṟpārviyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. ceṉṉi aracāṅkattār immuyaṟcikku oru laṭcam rūpā utaviyiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ipputiya pērakarātikku vēṇṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷ akarātikaḷilum vēṟu palavakikaḷilum cēkarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. viṉslōvāl yāḻppāṇa vaḻakkil māttiram uḷḷaṉa veṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭa 3000 coṟkaḷ kumāracuvāmippiḷḷi, muttuttampippiḷḷi ‘jāvaṉā kallūri’ (Jaffna college) puropasar haṭcaṉ mutalāṉavarkaḷ aṭaṅkiya oru capikku ārāyccikku viṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ nūṟṟukku 97 coṟkaḷ iṉṟum yāḻppāṇar vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉaveṉṟu oppuk koṇṭaṉar. putiya pērakarāti (lexicon)yil 83,000 allatu 84,000 coṟkaḷ variyil tāṉ iruttal kūṭum.
6. vaḻakku
“tamiḻmoḻi” vaḻakku1 eṉavum ceyyuḷ eṉavum irukūṟupaṭṭu iyaṅkukiṉṟatu. cāttā vā, nī poruḷit tēṭiṉāy, avaṉ mikap pacittāṉ, yāṉ cōṟu koṭuttēṉ eṉṟāṟpōla yāvarum vaḻaṅkum coṟṟoṭarkaḷ vaḻakku eṉappaṭum. iḻintōr palavāṟu citivupaṭak kūṟuvaṉavum oruvāṟṟāl vaḻakkēyāyiṉum avi aṟivuṭiyōrāl mēṟkoḷḷappaṭāvākum.
7. ceyyuḷ
“nallaṟivuṭiyārāl palavakic coṟkaḷālum amiti peṟac ceyyappaṭuvaṉa ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭum. pāṭṭukkaḷē yaṉṟic cūttiram uri mutalāyiṉavum ceyyuḷ eṉṉum peyarāl cūttirac ceyyuḷ eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiṉa. yām ippoḻutu ceyyuḷ eṉṉum peyarip pāṭṭukkē urittākkic cūttiram allātavikaḷi uri yeṉavum, pāṭṭu eṉavum iru vakippaṭuttik koḷkiṉṟōm. uri yiṉiyum palvakippaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. ceyyuṭ kaḷiṉ iṭiyē viravivarum uri cilappatikārattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu. ceyyum allatu cūttirap poruḷit teḷivuṟuttum urikaḷ tamiḻil mikkuḷḷaṉa. yātāṉumoru varalāṟu allatu uṇmiyiṉic ceyyu ḷiṉṟit toṭarcciyākak kūṟum urinaṭiyāṉiyaṉṟa nūl, ‘urinūl’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkaṟkuriyatu. attakiya nūl paṇṭic cāṉṟōr ceytatāka namakku oṉṟuṅ kiṭittilatu. urinaṭiyāṉatu payilvāratu aṟivu nuṇuki niṉṟu ārāytaṟ kēṟṟa tiṭpamum kūṟum poruḷukkēṟṟa ōci yum uṭiyataṉṟu. ātaliṉ pāṭṭukkaḷ pōla, uṇarviṉi eḻuppi iṉpañ ceyvatākātu. ōr variyaṟiyiṉmiyiṉ citiviṉṟi nili peṟuvatu mākātu. eṉiṉum eḷitiṟ poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatāyc curuṅkiya aṟiviṉarkkup payaṉṟaruvatu urinaṭiyē yākaliṉ, ovvoru moḻikkum urinūlkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa veṉpatiṟ ṟaṭiyilli. avvakiyil tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyuṟā tiruntatu iraṅkattakka toṉṟē.”
“tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ māṭci aḷaviṭaṟpālaṉa valla. piṟamoḻic ceyyuḷi yellām tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷ veṉṟu viṭṭaṉa eṉṉalām. nam muṉṉiyōr tam nuṇṇaṟivāṉum, uḻip pāṉuṅ kaṇṭaṟinta arumporuḷ maṇikaḷi yellām namakku utavu māṟu tokuttu vitta kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ pāṭṭukkaḷēyākum. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṟpaṭṭa nam periyōr eṉpu, tōl, narampukaḷāl yākkappaṭṭa uṭampiṉi yeṭuttu namakketiril vīṟṟirukka, nām avar kaḷōṭu, aḷavaḷāviyum, avarkaḷ kūṟum uṟuti moḻikaḷik kēṭṭum, aṟivum āṇmiyum uṭiyarāy iṉpuṟṟiruttaliṉum, peṟutaṟkariya pēṟu yātuḷatu? ettaṉiyō āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu viḷaṅki yiruntu piṉ māṟutaluṟṟu maṟintupōṉa mali, nilaṉ, kaṭal, yāṟu mutaliyaṉavellām pulavaruṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷil tam paṇṭiya vuruvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkutalik kāṇumiṭattu, namakkuṇṭāku muṇarcciyi yāteṉṟu kūṟalām? ulakameṉṉum kāṭci maṉṟattil uyarvaṟa vuyarnta oruvaṉāl kālantōṟum amittu vikkappaṭum eṇṇil pētamāṉa poruḷkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷiyum, moḻikaḷeṉṉum varivu kōl koṇṭu, uḷḷa meṉṉum kiḻiyil oḻuṅkuṟat tīṭṭi vitta ōviyarākum nallicip pulavarkaḷ, avvōviyak kāṭciyil uṟittu niṟkuṅkāl uṇṭāya uṇarcciyē vaṭivāka , aṉṉavar nam cennāvi ṉiṉṟeḻunta pāṭṭukkaḷiṉum namakkiṉpam viḷippaṉa yāviyuḷa? ‘urukip peruki yuḷaṅ kuḷira’ mukantu koṇṭu, parukaṟ kiṉiya paraṅ karuṇit taṭaṅkaṭal pōṉṟu parukuntōṟum parukuntōṟum vaṟṟāta pērūṟṟāyk kaḻipēriṉimi curantūṭṭuvaṉa pāṭṭukkaḷēyā meṉka.”
“ikkālattē poruṭ polivu ciṟitu miṉṟi, etuki, mōṉi yamiyac coṟkaḷit teṟṟivippatē pāṭṭeṉṟu karutik koṇ ṭiruppārum uḷar. maṭakkum tiripumākak coṟkaḷi aṭukki vittu. iraṇṭoru pōlippāṭṭiyaṟṟiṉārip pulavar variciyiṟ cērt teṇṇuvārumuḷar. antō! ivaṟṟukkellām kāraṇam pāṭṭiṉ taṉmi iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyāmiyēyākum. tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ‘nōkku’ mutaliya iṉṟiyamiyāc ceyyuḷuṟuppukkaḷi yoḻittu viṭṭup piṟkālattār yāppilakkaṇam vakuttatum, pāṭṭiṉṟaṉmi yaṟiyātu, piḻineṟiyiṟ piṟar celluvataṟkētuvāyiṟṟeṉka. ivaṟṟi yellām otukkic, curuṅkiya coṟkaḷil āḻntu akaṉṟa poruḷkaḷi yuṭiyaṉavāy, iyaṟki neṟi vaḻātaṉavāy, uṇarviṉi eḻuppi, meyppāṭu tōṟṟuvikkum cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷiyē aṟivuṭi yōr ‘pāṭṭu’ eṉṟu koḷvārāvar. attakiya pāṭṭukkaḷtām eṇṇiṟantaṉa vuḷa.” (kapilar-pak.84-87 paṇṭitar na.mu.vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār.)
“tamiḻmoḻiyiluḷḷa ceyyuḷōci amitiyē pā veṉṟu peyar peṟuvatām. oruvaṉ toliviṭattiruntu oru pāṭṭuppāṭa appāṭṭiṉ coṟporuḷ iṉṉaveṉṟu pulappaṭāvāyiṉum, avvōci varumāṟṟi uyttuṇarntu kāṇpāṉukku avaṉ pāṭuñ ceyyuḷ iṉṉa pāveṉṟu aṟiyakkiṭakkum, iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇiya vicumpiṉ kaṇ aliyaliyāy eḻumpi ōr oḻuṅkāka varum ōciyē pā vākumeṉat tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷuriyilē pērāciriyarum naṉku viḷakkiṉār. ivvāṟu tōṉṟip parampuñ ceyyuḷōciyit tamiḻāciriyar veṇpā, akavaṟpā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉa nālvakip paṭuttiṉār. piṉṉum innālvakip pāvi ṉōciyi nōkkumiṭattu veṇpāviṟ kalippāvum akavaṟpāvil vañcippāvum aṭaṅkuvaṉavām. iḵtu ‘āciriya naṭittē vañci yēṉi-veṇpā naṭittē kaliyeṉa moḻipa’(tol.cey-208) eṉṟum, ‘pāviru maruṅkiṉip paṇpuṟat tokuppiṉ, āciriyappā veṉpa veṇpā veṉṟāṅ, kāyirupāvi ṉuḷaṭaṅkumeṉpa’ (cey.107) eṉṟum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟumāṟṟāṉ viḷaṅkum. (paṭṭiṉappāli ārāycci-āciriyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ)
ceyyuḷāvatu “pāṭṭuri nūlē” (cey-78) eṉṉumceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya ēḻu nilamum aṟam mutaliya mūṉṟu poruḷum payappa nikaḻvatu”- nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
“vēṟṟu nāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum poruḷkaḷumāṉa āṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷ taṉṉi vantu aṇukappeṟāmal taṉ teyva vaḷa nāṭṭu āṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷiyē namatu taṇṭamiḻttāy taṉ mēṟkoṇṭu polintāḷ. annāḷil avaḷatu āmpaṟ cevvāyiṉiṉṟum amiḻtam oḻukiṉāṟpōl puṟam pōnta veṇpā akavaṟpā kalippā eṉṉum iyaṟkic centamiḻp pākkaḷ pāviṉaṅkaḷiṉ ariya periya amiti kaḷi yellām muṟṟaveṭuttu muṭiyaviḷakkum aruntamiḻ nūl olkāp perumpukaḻt tolkāppiya moṉṟumē yām.”
“iṉi annāṭ kaḻintapiṉ iṭippaṭṭa kālattē viḷi poruḷ vaḷaṉillā vēṟṟu nāṭṭavar vaṟumi kūrntu centamiḻt taṉi vaḷanāṭu pukuntār. pukutara, ellā makkaḷiṭattum aṉpuṭiyavaḷāṉa centamiḻt tāy ammakkaḷ tammiyum akam kaṉintu mukam malarntu ēṟṟu avar vaṟumitīrat taṉpāluḷḷavikaḷiyellām uḷaṅ kuḻintu utaviyūṭṭi avari makiḻvittaṉaḷ; makiḻvikka avarum tamatu miṭi tīrntu tām tamatu vaṟaṅkūr nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ koṇarnta paṉmoḻic cilpaṇikaḷit tamatu naṉṟikku aṟikuṟiyāka aṉṉi ki koṭuttār; nantamiḻaṉṉi avi iyalpāṉum aḷavāṉum mikac ciṟiyavāyiṉum aṉpuṟu makkaḷ tantaṉareṉpataṉāl uvarātu ēṟṟut taṉ vili varampaṟṟa maṇikkalaṉkaḷōṭu avaṟṟik kalantaṇintāḷ. immuṟi yāl vaṭamoḻiyāḷar koṇarnta cila coṟkaḷum yāppu vakikaḷumām aṇikalaṉkaḷum nam tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ puṉita mēṉiyiṉkaṭ kāṇap paṭalāyiṉa.
“iṉṉum ivviṭippaṭṭa kālattē vaṭamoḻi makkaḷ tiraḷ tiraḷākat tamiḻ vaḷanāṭu pukuntu centamiḻaṉṉi pālaṭikkalam pukavē, nantamiḻaṉṉi taṉ makkaḷpāl vitta aṉpiṉum miṭipaṭṭu vanta avvayaṟputalvarpāl aṉpu mika vittu avari makiḻvippāṉ vēṇṭi, avar māṟṟit tanta viruttap pākkaḷām aṇikalaṉkaḷit taṉakku icintavāṟṟāṟ pala vakippaṭat tirittuc ciṟakkaccey taṇintiṭuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. itaṉālaṉṟō ki.pi.mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil tikaḻnta civa camayācāriyarāṉa māṇikkavācakar kālantoṭṭu iṟṟi nāḷvariyum kāviyaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, itikācaṅkaḷ mutalāyiṉa vellām pala tiṟappaṭṭa viruttap pākkaḷiṉālēyē yākkappaṭuva vāyiṉa. (yāpparuṅkala virutti mukavuri.)
1. aṇi
aṇiyilakkaṇameṉa voṉṟu tolkāppiyattil taṉiyākak kūṟappaṭavilli. tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟiya uvamip pakutiyē ellāp pakutikaḷiyum taṉṉuḷaṭakki niṟkuñ ciṟappuṭiyatu. taṇṭiyalaṅkāram, vīracōḻiyam mutaliya piṟṟiñāṉṟi nūluṭiyār vaṭanūlār matamē paṟṟi uvamamoṉṟaṉiyē vāḷā pala peyarkaḷāṟ perukki avaṟṟāṉu mavi yaṭaṅkāmi kaṇṭu iḷippuṟṟār.
pērāciriyar uvamaviya luriyil, “iṉi ivvōttiṉuṭ kūṟu kiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiyum, collatikārattuḷḷum ceyyuḷiya luḷḷum collukiṉṟa cila poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅkikkoṇṭu, maṟṟavi ceyyuṭkaṇṇē aṇiyāmeṉa ikkālattu āciriyar nūl ceytāru muḷar. avi orutaliyākac ceyyuṭku aṇi yeṉṟu kūṟappaṭā. poruḷatikārattuḷ (varum) poruṭ pakutiyellām ceyyuṭ kaṇiyākalāṉ, avi pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟaṉa eṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟit tokuttu aṇi yeṉṟu kūṟātu, vēṟu cilavaṟṟi urittu aṇi yeṉṟu kūṟutal payaṉil kūṟṟām” eṉṟu piṟkālat taṇikaḷi maṟuttuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
“ipporuḷaṇikaḷiṟ pala uvamameṉṟa ōraṇiyiṉaṭiyākavē tōṉṟiṉa ”veṉpatu pallōr tuṇipu. āciriyar tolkāppiyarum ittuṇipiṉarpōlum. itu, vaṭamoḻi appiya tīkkataravarkaḷukkum uṭaṉpāṭātal, avaratu cittira mīmāñcik kūṟṟāṉ viḷaṅkum. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu.
“uvami yeṉṉun tavalaruṅ kūtti
palvakik kōlam pāṅkuṟap puṉintu
kāppiya varaṅkiṟ kaviṉuṟat tōṉṟi
yāppaṟi pulava ritayam
nīppaṟu makiḻcci pūppa naṭikkumē”
“āyiṉum ulakil vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷevaṟṟilum uvami yoṉṟē aṭippaṭṭu naṭantiyalvatu veḷḷiṭi vilaṅkalām. ēṉiya avvoṉṟil niṉṟē kiḷippaṉa. itupaṟṟi yaṉṟō tolkāppiya ṉārum uvamaviya loṉṟē kūṟip pōntār” (tamiḻ celvak kēcavarāya mutaliyār)
2. poruḷ
poruṭ pakutiyil akam puṟam eṉṉum iraṇṭoḻukkaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkālattu āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāṉ makkaḷiṉ maṇa muṟikaḷiyum avar illaṟa voḻukkaṅkaḷi yum kūṟuvatu akam. akkālattu aracar ceyta pōr muṟikaḷiyum avarkaḷatu piṟa oḻukkaṅkaḷiyum kūṟuvatu puṟam. ākavē, poruḷati kāram paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ varalāṟāka vamintuḷḷatu. poruḷilak kaṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭum poruḷkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eṉṟeṉṟikkum kiṭikkak kūṭāta taṉit tamiḻk karuttukkaḷākum.
“taḷḷāp poruḷiyalpiṟ taṇṭamiḻāy vantilār
koḷḷārik kuṉṟu payaṉ” (paripāṭal)
“ilippuṟaṅ kaṇṭakaṇṇi yiṉṟami ḻiyaṟkiyiṉpam”
“tēvar paṇṇiya tīntoṭi yiṉcuvi
mēvar teṉtamiḻ meypporu ḷātaliṉ” (cintāmaṇi)
eṉṟa paripāṭal cintāmaṇi eṉṉum paḻantamiḻp paṉuvalkaḷiṉiṉṟum kāṭṭappaṭṭa aṭikaḷ poruḷ ilakkaṇam tamiḻmoḻikkē contam eṉpati valiyuṟuttum.
3. eṇvaki maṇam
“maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟa leṭṭaṉuḷ”(tol kaḷa-ka) eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl akkālattu vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya maṇa muṟikaḷ eṭṭu eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm1
“aṟanili yoppē poruḷkōḷ teyvam
yāḻōr kūṭṭa marumporuḷ viṉiyē
irākkatam pēynili yeṉṟik kūṟiya
maṟiyōr maṉṟa leṭṭivi”
eṉṉum iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷuri mēṟkōḷāl ammaṉṟalkaḷ eṭṭiṉ peyarkaḷ pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. immaṇamuṟikaḷ ulaka makkaḷ ellōriṭiyum kāṇappaṭṭavi.
(1) aṟanili (piramam) yāvatu: ōrirutu kaṇṭa kaṉṉiyi maṟṟi yirutu kāṇāmē koḷaṟpāla marapiṉōrkku nīr peytu koṭuttal. eṉṉi?
“oppāruk koppā rorupūp pirintapiṉ
iṉpāṉ matitōṉṟā vellikka-ṇappāṟ
ṟarumamē pōlkeṉṟu takkārkkuc cērttal
piramamām pōlum peyar”
eṉṟārākaliṉ,
“piramamāvatu otta kōttirattāṉāy nāṟpatteṭṭi yāṇṭu piramacariyaṅ kāttāṉukkup pūppeytiya paṉṉīrāṭṭip pirāyattāḷi aṇikalaṉaṇintu muttī muṉṉark koṭuppatu” eṉa uriyāciriyarkaḷ kūṟuvar. nāṟpatteṭṭiyāṇṭup pirāyattāṉukkup paṉṉīrāṭṭip peṇṇi maṇamuṭippatu takutiyāṉa maṇamuṟiyākak kāṇappaṭa villi. im maṇattippaṟṟik kūṟum paḻiya pāṭal nāṟpatteṭṭi yāṇṭu piramacariyaṅkāttal mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟi yātum kūṟāmiyāl, apporuntā maṇamuṟi āriya nāṭṭār vaḻakkēyākum. immuṟi yiṉiyē avarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu aṟanili vaḻakkōṭu oppavittup piramam eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ pōlum.
oppu (vitimaṇam allatu piracāpattiyam) āvatu, koṭutta pariyattiṉ irumaṭaṅku makaṭ koṭuppōṉ koṭuttal. eṉṉi?
“koṭutta poruḷ vāṅkik koṇṭapēr 
maṭuppar maṭuttaṟ kamintā-laṭuppō
ṉiraṇṭā maṭaṅkupey tīva tatuvē
yiraṇṭā maṇatti ṉiyalpu”
eṉṟārākaliṉ.
poruḷkōḷ (āriṭam) āvatu: “ēṟum āvum koṇarntu niṟīi, avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar kikku nīr peytu koṭuttal. eṉṉi?
“iṟkulattō ṭoppāṉuk koppā ṉimilēṟāp 
poṟkuḷampiṟ poṟkōṭṭa vāppuṉintu-muṟpaṭuttu
vāriṭampē rāmuliyi vāḻkkikkaṇ vippaturi
āriṭam pērā mataṟku”
eṉṟārākaliṉ.
teyvam āvatu: vēḷvi āciriyaṉ oruvaṟku makaḷi aṇikala ṉaṇintu avvēḷvittī muṉṉark koṭuppatu, eṉṉi?
“meyppālip peṇṭaṉmi yeytiyapiṉ melliyali
yoppa vuṇarnta poḻutuṇṭa-loppārkku
neytayaṅku tīmuṉṉar nēriḻiyi yīvatē
teyvappē rākun terintu”
eṉṟārākaliṉ,
yāḻōr kūṭṭam (kantaruvam) āvatu: “otta kulaṉum, kuṇaṉum, aḻakum, aṟivum, paruvamum uṭiyār, yārumil oru ciṟikkaṇ aṉpu mītūrat tāmē puṇarntu oḻukum oḻukkam. eṉṉi?
‘otta kulattār tamiyarā yōriṭattut
tattamiṟ kaṇṭatum maṉpiṉā-luyttiṭa
vantara miṉṟip puṇarva tatuvarō
kantaruva meṉṟa karuttu’
eṉavum,
“muṟcey viṉiyatu muṟiyā vuṇmiyi
ṉotta viruvaru muḷḷaka nekiḻntu
kāṭci yiyan terital tēṟṟaleṉa
nāṉkiṟan tavaṭku nāṇu maṭaṉum
accamum payirppu mavaṟku
muyirttakat taṭakkiya,
aṟivu niṟivu mōrppun tēṟṟamum
maṟiyavarkku māṇṭatō riṭattiṉ
meyyuṟu vakiyum muḷḷalla tuṭampuṟappaṭāt
tamiḻiyal vaḻakkameṉat
taṉṉaṉpu miki perukiya
kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu kantaruva maṇamē”
eṉṟār avinayaṉār.
arumporuḷ viṉi (akaram) āvatu: “iṉṉatu ceytārkku ivaḷuriyaḷ eṉṟa iṭattu, aṉṉatu ceyteytuvatu, avi villēṟṟutal, tiripaṉṟiyeytal, kollēṟu kōṭal mutaliya.”
irākkatam āvatu: “āṭimē liṭutal, pūmēliṭutal, katavaṭit tal mutaliyavaṟṟāl valitiṟkōṭal.
pēynili yāvatu: “tuñciṉārōṭum, mayaṅkiṉārōṭum, carittārōṭum, cettārōṭum, vilaṅkiṉōṭum, iḻitaku marapil yārumillā voruciṟikkaṇ puṇarntu oḻuku moḻukkam.”
tolkāppiyar teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷ cilavaṟṟi vaṭanāṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷōṭu oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. tolkāppiyar kuṟippiṭṭa yāḻōr ‘kantaruvar’ eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉarāvar. ivarkaḷ imaya mali aṭivāraṅkaḷil vāḻum aḻakiya tōṟṟamuṭiya makkaḷ eṉṟum, ivarkaḷuḷ āṭavarum makaḷirum oḻukkattāl ciṟitu taḷarntavarkaḷ eṉṟum vittiyā eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. tolkāppiyar tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaḷavu oḻukkam kantaruvar vaḻakkatti orupuṭi okkumeṉa uṇarttiṉār. tolkāppiyattil varum antaṇar, maṟi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, cilaviṭaṅkaḷil āriyap pirāmaṇariyum avar vētaṅkaḷi yum cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. vīramāmuṉivar kiṟittuva vētattiyum, kiṟittuva kurumāriyum kuṟikka vētam,vētiyar eṉṉum coṟkaḷi āṇṭuḷḷār. tolkāppiyattil varum maṟi, maṟiyōr, antaṇar eṉṉum coṟkaḷukku iṭamaṟintu poruḷ koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
4. akam
“akameṉpatu, talivaṉun taliviyum tammuḷotta aṉpiṉa rāyk kūṭuṅ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ piṟanta iṉpam akkūṭṭattiṉkaṇ āṇṭaṉupavitta akkālattil iṉpam evvāṟiruntateṉṟavviruvari loruvari yoruvar kēṭkiṉ, tamakkup pulappaṭātāy uḷḷat tuṇarvē nukarvatāy akattē nikaḻtaliṉ, akameṉap peyarāyiṟṟu. akameṉiṉum kāmap pakuti yeṉiṉum okkum.” akattic cārntuvarum poruḷellām akapporuḷām.
5. kaḷavu
“uruvun tiruvuṅ kulamum mutaliyavaṟṟāl oppuyarvaṟṟu viḷaṅkun talimakaṉoruvaṉ vēṭṭiyāṭik koṇṭu maliccāral oṉṟaṉ pakkattē taṉiyaṉāy vantāṉāka, āṅkut tiṉippuṉaṅkāttut tōḻiyarōṭu viḷiyāṭupavaḷum uru tiru mutaliyavaṟṟāl oppuyarvaṟṟa vaḷumākiya malivāṇar makaḷorutti viḷiyāṭṭu viruppāṟ ṟōḻiyar taṉit taṉi pirinta niliyil tāṉun taṉiyaḷāy attalivaṉ muṉpu etirppaṭṭaṉaḷ. etirppaṭṭa iruvarum ūḻvayattāl uḷḷaṅ kavarntu avviṭattē makiḻntu piṉṉar oruvarkkoruvar pirivāṟṟātu oruvāṟu āṟṟuvittukkoṇṭu tammiṭam nōkkik ceṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu vitiyāṟ kūṭṭappaṭṭut pirinta talimakaṉ tāṉ kātaluṟṟa taliviyit tirumpak kāṇumavāviṉāl tūṇṭappaṭṭu, muṉṉāṭ kūṭiya iṭatti nāṭic cella, aṅṅaṉamē aṉpu tūṇṭa āṅku vanta taliviyit talimakaṉ etirppaṭṭuk kaṇṭu makiḻntu kātalāl muṉpōlak kalantāṉ. immuṟi mūṉṟām muṟi kūṭum pōtu taṉ pāṅkaṉāl talivi yiruppiṭamaṟintu ceṉṟu avaḷic cērvaṉ. iṉit tōḻiyāl eppoḻutuñ cūḻappaṭṭuḷḷa taliviyi iṅṅaṉamē palakālun taṉiyiṭat tetirppaṭṭuk kūṭutal arumiyāyiruttal paṟṟit taliviyit taṭiyiṉṟi aṉupavikkak karutiyavaṉāy ataṟkut takka tuṇiyāvāḷ avaḷ kātaṟṟōḻiyē eṉpati yaṟintu koṇṭu avaḷpāṟ ceṉṟu kuṟippākavum veḷippaṭiyākavum taṉ kuṟikūṟi vēṇṭa, avaḷ palapaṭiyāka ārāyntu iruvarukkuṅ kūṭṭamuṇmi yuṇarntu, iṉi nāmum itaṟku uṭaṉpaṭalē nalam eṉṟu avviruvari yuṅ kūṭṭuvataṟku muyalvaḷ. iṅṅaṉam tōḻiyāl palamuṟi kūṭivaru miṭiyil taliviyāl kākkappaṭṭuvarun tiṉippuṉam muṟṟik katir koytalēṉum piṟa kāraṇamēṉum nikaḻat talivikku akkāval nīṅki maṉiyakattē taṅkumpaṭi nērtalum, akkālattu iravil tōḻi avviruvariyuṅ kūṭṭa muyalvaḷ. iṅṅaṉam nikaḻum kaḷavoḻukkam ciṟukac cilar palarkkut teriyavantu nāḷaṭivil ūril pēralarāy eḻumpa, atu kāraṇattāṟ peṟṟōr taliviyi iṟceṟittut takkapaṭi kāvaṟ paṭuttuvar. ataṉāl talimakaṉik kāṇappeṟāta āṟṟāmi talivi māṭṭup perukutal kaṇṭu, tōḻi, iṉik kaṟpu neṟippaṭṭa ivaḷ iṟantu paṭavuṅ kūṭumeṉṟu karuti, itaṟku vēṟu upāyamoṉṟuṅ kāṇāta niliyil, avaḷ talivaṉiyaṭintu ‘iṉi nīr ivaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇantu koḷvatē kāriyam’ eṉṟu kūṟi oruvarum aṟiyāvaki taliviyi veḷiyēṟṟit talimakaṉpāṟ cērppikka, avaṉ avaḷi uṭaṉaḻittukkoṇṭu, taṉṉūr nōkkic celvāṉ; cellumiṭiyil, taliviyiṉ cuṟṟattār akkaḷavaṟintu vantu talivaṉi mēṟcellāvāṟu taṭuttum, avaḷ taṉ kātal muḻutum talivaṉ pāṟ cārtti niṟpatu kaṇṭu ‘ivaḷ kaṟpu neṟippaṭṭāḷ’ eṉṟu aṟintukoṇṭu avviruvarkkun tīṅku ceyyātu tirumpip pōvar. pōkat talivaṉuṭaṉ makiḻntu ceṉṟu talivi avaṉūraṭintu vāḻ vaḷeṉa uṇarka.” -tol-poruḷ. ārāycci . mu. irākava aiyaṅkār
ikkaḷa voḻukkam paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kūttar kūttiyarkaḷāl vēttaviyil apinayittu naṭikkap paṭṭu vantaṉa. “vēttiyal akam potuviyal puṟameṉpārum piṟitu kūṟuvāru muḷar’ (aṭiyārkku nallār). akkālattu nāṭaka vaḻakkiyum ulakiyal vaḻakkiyum taḻuvic ceyyappaṭṭatē tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum kaḷaviyalākum. “ittamiḻ nāṭakat tamiḻ eṉappaṭum; kiḷavi oḻuṅkupaṭak kōttuk katipōla vantu nāṭakattuk kēṟṟaliṉ” eṉak kūṟuvar tañcivāṇaṉ kōvi urikārar.
6. kaṟpu
taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōyt talivaṉ taṉṉūrilēṉum taliviyiṉ cuṟṟattār cammatittu vēṇṭa, avaḷūrilēṉum avaḷi vitippaṭi maṇantukoṇṭu avviruvarum illiruntu āṟṟum aṟavoḻukkam kaṟpeṉappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam illiruntu illaṟam naṭātti iṉpuṟṟirukkum nāḷil cila kāraṇatti muṉṉiṭṭut talivaṉ taliviyi viṭṭup pirintu celvaṉ. aṅṅaṉam taliviyi viṭṭup pirital aṟanūli ōtutaṟ poruṭṭāvatu, nāṭukāttaṟ poruṭṭāvatu, māṟupaṭṭa iruvēntaric cantu ceytaṟporuṭṭāvatu, vēntarkkut tuṇiyāyp pōr ceytaṟporuṭṭāvatu, poruḷīṭṭum poruṭ ṭāvatu, parattiyar poruṭṭāvatu nikaḻum. “munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūu vōṭilli” eṉpatāl peṇmakkaḷi marakkalattil ēṟṟic cellal paḻiya nāḷ marapaṉṟu. ōtaṟ pirivu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷum maṟṟap pirivukaḷellām ōr āṇṭukkuḷḷum muṟṟuppeṟa vēṇṭumeṉpa.
talimakaṉ pirintu cellumiṭattut talimakaḷ avaṉ pirivāṟ ṟātu iraṅkuvāḷ. talimakaṉu miraṅki avaḷit tēṟṟi iṉṉa irutuvil iṉṉa tiṅkaḷil yāṉ mīṇṭu varuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟic cila kuṟikaḷuñ ceytu viṭṭuc celvāṉ. talimakaḷum talivaṉ coṟpiḻiyātu illiruntu illaṟamāṟṟi varuvāḷ, avaṉ kuṟitta paruvam vanta viṭattu avaṉ piriviṉi āṟṟātu varuntuvāḷ. tōḻi avaḷ āṟṟāmiyik kaṇṭu varuntit talivaṉ kuṟitta paruvam aḵtaṉṟēl avaṉ ceyta kuṟikaḷ vantamiyāṉ avaṉ vārāniṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟum piṟavuṅ kūṟit taliviyi āṟṟuvippāḷ. talivaṉun tāṉ kuṟittuc ceṉṟa viṉi muṭittuk kuṟitta paruvam varavē mīṇṭu varuvāṉ. mīḷum vaḻiyil tāṉ ceyta kuṟikaḷellān tōṉṟum; atu poruṭṭut talivi āṟṟā ḷeṉṟu pākaṟkuk kūṟit tēriviriviṟ celuttac ceytu kaṭukivantu talimakaḷi yeyti viruntayarvāṉ. piṉṉar iruvarum pirintirunta viṭattu nikaḻnta ceytikaḷiyellām oruvarukkoruvar collik koṇṭu iṉpuṟuvar.
iṅṅaṉam illaṟam naṭāttit talivaṉun taliviyum veṟukku maḷavum kāmaviṉpan tuyttu makkaḷip peṟṟu mutumi yeytiyapōtu tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu meyyuṇarntu vīṭupeṟutalē ivvoḻukkattiṉ muṭinta poruḷām.
“kāmañ cāṉṟa kaṭikkōṭ kāli
yēmañ cāṉṟa makkaḷōṭu tuvaṉṟi
yaṟampuri cuṟṟamoṭu kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyuñ
ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē.” -tol.kaṟ.51
kātalvaḻic cellumuḷḷamum iṟivaṉi naccumuḷḷamum orupāṉmiya vākaliṉ, aṉpiṉ māṇpiṉi viḷakkum akappakuti viḻumiteṉa aṟiñar uccimēl vittup pōṟṟuvar. iṟivaṉ pakkuvam vāynta nalluyirkaḷi āṭkoḷḷum muṟiyiṉi māṇikkavācaka cuvāmikaḷ “kāmañ cāṉṟa ñāṉap paṉuva” lākiya tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyār eṉṉum akapporuḷ nūlāl viḷakkiyiruttal karuttil ūṉṟat takkatu.
7. puṟam
“akampōl otta aṉpuṭiyār tāmēyaṉṟi ellārālum tuyttu uṇarappaṭutalāṉum, ivvāṟiruntateṉap piṟarkkuk kūṟappaṭuta lāṉum itu puṟamāyiṟṟu.
itaṉi veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇ ākiya ēḻu tiṇikaḷāka vakuttuk kūṟuvar. piṟkālattavar karanti nocci mutaliya pala tiṇikaḷiyuṅ kūṭṭi itaṉiyē paṉṉi raṇṭu tiṇikaḷāka virittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivvēḻaṉuḷ kāñci pāṭāṇ ṭiṇiyiraṇṭiṉum ulakiṉ niliyiṉmiyiyum, talivaṉip pukaḻtal kāriyattip paṟṟiyuṅ kūṟuvar. ēṉiya aintiṇikaḷum akkālap pōrttiṟaṉi viḷakkuvaṉavām. paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaḷ pōr ceytaṟku ēkuṅkāl ceyyum camar campantamāṉa vevvēṟu pūkkaḷic cūṭip porutal vaḻakkamāyiruntamiyiṉ, avvap pūkkaḷiṉ peyarāṉē oḻukkattiyum kūṟiṉar tolkāppiyar.
“iruperuvēntar tammuḷ poruvatu karutiyak kāl oruvar maṟṟavar nāṭṭiluḷḷa āniriyik kavartal kuṟittut tamatu taṇṭat talivar, muṉippulaṅkāppōr mutaliyōri ēvuvar. aṉṉōr paṭikaḷik kūṭṭi nalvāyppuḷ mutaliya naṟcakuṉa moḻikaḷik kēṭṭu, oṟṟaṟiya vallāri muṉṉar ēvi, avar ēviyavāṟē tuppaṟintu piṉ ceṉṟu niri niṟkum pulaṅkaḷukkarukē maṟintoḻukip piṉ oyyeṉak kiḷampi niri kāttōrik koṉṟu vīḻtti āniriyi metteṉavāy uṭaṉkoṇṭu tam nāṭu celluvar. celluṅkāl tammit toṭarntu poravantārip piṉ aṇiyattār tāmē niṉṟu porutu vīḻtta muṉ ceṉṟār āniriyi iṉpuṭaṉ naṭattic celvar. iṅṅaṉam etirttavariyellām māyttavaḻi, āniriyit tam ūrppuṟattē koṇṭuvantu niṟutti, ūriluḷḷōrkkum, iravalarkkum avaṟṟip pakuttuk koṭuttut tām koṇṭa veṟṟik kaṟikuṟiyāka uṇṭāṭṭayarntu kaḷippar. niri paṟikoṭuttōrum iṅṅaṉamē porutu niri mīṭṭup pōtalumuṇṭu. ivviraṇṭum veṭci yeṉṟār tolkāppiyar. piṟṟi nāṭ pōntōr niri mīṭṭalik karanti yeṉap piṟitoru peyarāṟ kūṟuvar.
“iṅṅaṉam pacuttiraḷik kavarntapiṉ peruvēntaṉ nāṭu koḷḷum uḷḷattaṉāy nimittikaṉ nalla nāḷeṉṟu kūṟiya nāḷilē taṉ kuṭi, vāḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip puṟappaṭac ceytu, eḷḷuruṇṭi, tiṉimā, pori, avari, tuvari, niṇam, kuṭar, utiram mutaliyaṉa niṟinta tāḻiyik koṟṟaviyiṉ poruṭṭu muṉ eṭuttuc cellap piṉṉart taṉ cēṉiyōṭu puṟappaṭṭu māṟṟāṉ tēcattiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuppāṉ. appōtu vīramuracam atirum; yāṉikaḷ vīṟiṭum; cēṉikaḷ āravārañ ceyyum. aracaṉ taṉ cēṉikaṭku vēṇṭiya āyutaṅkaḷik koṭup pāṉ. mēlum taṉ paṭi muḻuvatukkum oruvaṉit talivaṉākkit taṉ paṭivīrar yāvarum avaṉ coṟpaṭiyē kēṭṭu naṭakkumāṟu paṇippāṉ. pōr muṭivil, pōril ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya vīrarkku ēṉāti, kāviti mutaliya ciṟappup paṭṭaṅkaḷic cūṭṭalumuṇṭu. aracaṉ taṉ paṭivīrarōṭu amarntu uṇṭu kaḷittalum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. aṉṉiya nāṭṭu aracaṉ tiṟikoṭukkac cammatippāṉāyiṉ, attiṟiyi vāṅkikkoṇṭu mīṇṭuviṭutalu muṇṭu. aṉṟēl, tāpatap paḷḷikaḷ antaṇar illaṅkaḷ kōvilkaḷ nīṅkalākap pakivar nāṭṭūrkaḷic cuṭṭerit taḻittalu muṇṭu. tamakkup puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōṭuvōrip pōrvīrar veṭṭāmal viṭuvar. pōril puṇpaṭṭa vīrari aracaṉum uṭaṉvīrarum pukaḻntu koṇṭāṭuvar. muṭivil aracaṉ taṉ vīraruṭ ciṟantārukku muttāram mutaliyaṉa koṭuppāṉ. cilarukkup parampari vāḻkkiyāka marutanila ūrkaḷ koṭuppatumuṇṭu.
akkālattil naṉṟāy araṇ ceyyappaṭṭa kōṭṭikaḷu miruntaṉa. pakiyaracar ik kōṭṭikaḷi etirttup porutalumuṇṭu. kōṭṭikaḷ, mali, kāṭu, nīr mutaliyaṉa illāta uḷnāṭṭil kal, ceṅkal mutaliyavaṟṟālamikkappaṭṭa matilāl vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kōṭṭikku veḷiyē vekutūram vari kāvaṟkāṭu parantirukkum. ik kāvaṟkāṭāṉatu vañcaṉi pala vāyttut tōṭṭi muḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl patikkappaṭṭatu. ik kāvaṟkāṭṭi yakaṉṟu ceṉṟāl matiliyaṭuttu akaṉṟu āḻnta akaḻ uṇṭu. itil karāṅkaḷum cuṟākkaḷum mikun tirukkum. itiyaṭuttu amikkappaṭṭatē puṟamatil. puṟamatiliṉ mēṟ puṟam pala poṟikaḷ, pataṇam, meyppuḻi , ñāyil mutaliyaṉavamintu eḻuvum cīppumuṭiya vāyiṟ kōpuramuṭittu. kōṭṭi matilil vēṟu pala iyantiraṅkaḷum uṇṭu. itaṟku uṭpuṟat tuḷḷatē uḷmatil. cila kōṭṭikaḷ mali araṇ, nila araṇ mutaliyaṉa cūḻntiruntaṉa. mēliruntu ampu eyyum patukkiṭaṅkaḷ matilakattē amintirukkum.
ivvāṟu kāval ceyyappaṭṭa kōṭṭiyip piṭippataṟku etir aracaṉ cēṉikaḷōṭu varuvāṉ. cēṉikaḷukku muṉ aracaṉiṉ koṭi cellum. pōr toṭaṅkum pōtu yāṉippaṭi muṉṉum, tēr kutirip paṭikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉum, kālāṭpaṭi kaṭiyilumākac cellum. kālāṭkaḷ māṭṭut tōlāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kēṭayatti iṭakkarattilum īṭṭi allatu kaṇṭakōṭāliyi valakkarattilum tāṅkiyiruppar. villāḷar iṭakkiyil villiyum mutukil ampaṟāt tūṇiyiyum vittiruppar. īṭṭippōr viṟpōr ceyvōrkaḷ orumār nīḷamuḷḷatum akaṉṟatum alakuḷḷatumākiya vāḷ tāṅki niṟpar; kutirimaṟavar kālāṭkaḷilum pārkka, ilēcāṉa āyutaṅ kaḷiyum kuṟukiya paricikaḷiyuṅ koṇṭu celvar. yāṉikaḷ eppoḻutum paṭiyiṉ perum pakutiyāyiruntatu. ivvakiyāṉa māṟṟaracaṉiṉ paṭi kōṭṭiyi yaṇukā muṉṉarē kōṭṭi mēlirukkum kōṭṭik kāvalar ampu māri poḻivar. avvampu māriyik kiṭukup paṭiyārum, kēṭakap paṭiyārum taṭuttum, tāṅkiyum etirnōkkic ceṉṟu kōṭṭiyik kiṭṭuvar. matil cūḻnta kiṭaṅkiṉ irukariyilum niṉṟu perum pōr purivār. perum palakikaḷi nīril mitakkaviṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ mītu ēṇikaḷic cārttik kōṭṭi mītēṟip porutalumuṇṭu. kōṭṭik kāvalar kōṭṭiyiṉ puṟampōntu pōr ceytalumuṇṭu. puṟamatilik kippaṟṟiya pakiyaracaṉ uḷmatili yum vaḷittu avvāṟē porutu kāvalāḷarik kōṭṭikkuḷ turattuvāṉ. uḷmatilum pakiyaracaṉ kippaṭavē, pōr ūriṉkaṇ nikaḻum. appoḻutu ūriluḷḷa kōyiṟ puricikaḷilum, matilkaḷilum vīṭukaḷ mītum ēṟi niṉṟu pōr ceyvār. etirttu vanta aracaṉ veṟṟi peṟuvaṉēl tōṟṟavēntaṉ peyarāl tāṉ muṭicūṭi maṅkala nīrāṭit taṉ vāḷukkum nīrāṭṭuc caṭaṅku ceytu, parantu niṉṟa taṉ paṭiyāri yellām oruṅku kūṭṭi, avaravarkkut takkac ciṟappuc ceytalumuṇṭu. pakivaṉ kōṭṭiyi muḻuvatum aḻittuk kaḻuti ēriṭṭuḻutu, uṇṇāvarakum vēlum vitittut tirumpuvatum, tiṟivāṅkit tirumpuvatum, aṉṟēl taṉ āṇiyi avviṭattum niliniṟuttu māṟu cila mātaṅtaṅki nāṭṭip paṇpaṭuttic cīr ceytuviṭṭut tirumpuvatum vaḻakkamāyiruntaṉa.
tamiḻ aracarkaḷ tōṟṟavarkaḷiṭam irakkamillātiruntaṉar. tōṟṟa aracaṉukkuriya ceḻippuḷḷa vayalkaḷum cōlikaḷum pāḻ nilaṅkaḷākkappaṭṭaṉa. aḻakiya araṇmaṉikaḷa āntiyum pēyum vāḻiṭaṅkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ periya tūṇkaḷ mata yāṉikaḷ kaṭṭum taṟikaḷāyiṉa. vicālitta maṭippaḷḷikaḷ, ānti mēlē iruntalaṟat tiruṭar koḷḷi koṇṭa poruḷip paṅkiṭum iṭaṅkaḷāyiṉa: nāṭṭiluḷḷār nālāpakkaṅkaḷilum ōṭip piḻittaṉar. aḻakiya ōviyan tīṭṭappaṭṭa cuvarkaḷiyuṭiya araṇmaṉikaḷ tarimaṭṭa mākkappaṭṭaṉa.
matilakattaṉṟic camaveḷiyoṉṟil iṭaṅkuṟittu aṅkē iruperu vēntar cēṉiyum etirūṉṟi niṉṟu pōr ceyyum vaḻakkamu miruntatu. tāṉi, yāṉi, kutiri,tēr mutaliya nālvakip paṭiyum aṇivakuttu niṉṟu caṇṭi ceyyum. aracaṉum cēṉiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu uṭaṉ niṉṟē pōr ceyvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. ippōril cēṉit talivar kāṭṭum vīram viyakkattakkatu. taṉ cēṉi murintōṭum camayamāyiruppiṉ cēṉittalivaṉ tāṉē cēṉikku muṉṉiṉṟu etiri paṭi muḻuvatiyum tāṉē tāṅkit taṭuttu niṉṟu taṉ paṭiyāḷari uṟcākappaṭuttic caṇṭikkut tūṇṭuvāṉ. taṉ aracaṉi māṟṟāṉ paṭicūḻak kāṇiṉ, yāṇṭiruntum paṭittalivar ōṭōṭiyum vantu avaṉi viṭuvippār. irupuṟamum vāṭpōrum maṟ pōrum vīrattuṭaṉ naṭipeṟum. ivvāṟāya caṇṭiyil irutiṟattārum okka maṭitalu muṇṭu. caṇṭi ceyyum iru vēntarum maṭivarēl avar maṉiviyar tīppukuvatum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu.
māṟṟārmītu pōr toṭaṅkumpōtu, āṉaṉiya antaṇar kuḻanti peṇ mutaliyōrip pōr nilattiṉiṉṟum vilakumāṟu aṟivuṟutti, aṉṉār avaṇiruntu vilakiya piṉṉar ampumāri corivatu tamiḻ vīrarkaḷatu vaḻakkāyiruntatu.
itaṉi,
“āvum āṉiyaṟ pārppaṉa mākkaḷum
peṇṭirum piṇiyuṭi yīrum pēṇit
teṉpula vāḻnark karuṅkaṭaṉ iṟukkum
poṉpōṟ putalvarp peṟāa tīrum
emmampu kaṭiviṭutum nummaraṇ cērmiṉeṉa
aṟattāṟu nuvalum pūṭki”
eṉa varūum puṟappāṭṭā ṉaṟika.
“veṭci nirikavartaṉ mīṭṭal karantiyām
vaṭkārmēṟ celvatu vañciyā-muṭkā
retirūṉṟal kāñci yeyilkātta ṉocci
yatuvaḷitta lāku muḻiñi-yatirap
poruvatu tumpiyām pōrkkaḷattu mikkōr
ceruveṉ ṟatuvāki yām” - piṅkalam
8. vīrakkal
pōril evarāṉum matikkappaṭum vīraṉāy viḷaṅki uyirviṭṭa vaṉi ekkālamum ñāpakappaṭuttumāṟu akkālattavar ceytirunta ēṟpāṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. vīrar akkālattu atikamāy matikkappaṭṭu vantaṉar. vīrac ceyalkaḷil viḷaṅkiya vīrari ekkālamum ñāpakappaṭut tum cātaṉaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttuvatum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. iṟanta vīraṉi niṉivukūrtaṟkākak kaṟṟūṇi niṟutti atil avvīraṉiṉ peyariyum avaṉ ceyta vīrac ceyalkaḷiyum veṭṭi akkallukku nīrāṭṭi, eṇṇeyip pūci, atil ammaṟavaṉiyē teyvamāka nāṭṭic ciṟappuc ceytu aviṉt teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiyatōṭum amiyāmal ak kallukkuk kōyilamittu matil, vāyil mutaliyaṉa kaṭṭi ālayaṅ kaḷākki avvīraṉip pōṟṟiyum vantaṉar.
“eṉṉimuṉ nillaṉmiṉ ṟevvir palareṉṉi
muṉṉiṉṟu kaṉṉiṉ ṟavar” - kuṟaḷ
iraṇṭāyira māṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē tam vīrari ñāpakappaṭuttu māṟu iṅṅaṉam kalnaṭṭup pēr poṟittu vitta nam muṉṉōr taṉmi ekkālattum viyakkat takkatākavē yirukkiṉṟatu.
9. iyaṟṟamiḻ
“tamiḻ iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttiṟattiṉatāyt toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.” iyaṟṟamiḻeṉpatu tamiḻar yāvarmāṭṭum potumiyiṉ ulaka vaḻakku, ceyyuḷ vaḻakku eṉṉum iruvaki vaḻak kiṉum iyaṅkukiṉṟa vacaṉamuñ ceyyuḷumākiya nūlkaḷiṉ tokutiyām. itaṉuḷ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkum. caṅkac ceyyuḷ kaḷum, urikaḷum, purāṇam mutaliya ēṉiya nūlkaḷum iyaṟṟamiḻē yām. iyaṟṟamiḻ ēṉi icittamiḻ nāṭakattamiḻ ākiya iraṇṭaṟkum muṉṉart tōṉṟiṉamipaṟṟi mutaṟkaṇ vikkappaṭṭatākum. ēṉiya iraṇṭum iyaṟṟamiḻiṉṟi iyaṅkum vaṇmiyuṭiyaṉavalla.
“iyaṟṟamiḻiṉuḷḷē eḻuttuc col poruḷ yāppu aṇikaḷum, icittamiḻiṉuḷḷē ēḻu curaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṟ piṟakkum paṇkaḷiṉ vakiyum piṟavum, nāṭakat tamiḻiṉuḷḷē kūttu vikaṟpamum apinaya vikaṟpamum kūttiyarilakkaṇamum araṅkilakkaṇamum piṟavum viḷakkap paṭṭaṉavām.” -pālāmirutam-civappirakāca paṇṭitar.
“vaḻakkiyalum vaḻakkiyalāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ceyyuḷiyalum paṟṟi yeḻunta ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻeṉappaṭum. acceyyuḷiṉ iṉṟi yamiyāta ici iciyilakkaṇameṉap peyareyti avviyaṟṟamiḻp piṉṉar vikkappaṭṭateṉappaṭum; ivviraṇṭaṉvaḻi nikaḻttuṅ kūttilakkaṇamākiya nāṭakat tamiḻ avaṟṟuṭ piṉṉarttāmeṉa muṟimi kūṟutalum.” -pērāciriyar uri.
10. icittamiḻ
“iyaṟṟamiḻ paṇṇōṭu kalantu tāḷattōṭu naṭipeṟiṉ icittamiḻām.” orukāl tamiḻakattu icikkalvi mika uyarnili aṭintiruntateṉpatip paḻiya nūlkaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. akattiyar irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇitta varalāṟum itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum. irāvaṇaṉiṉ icikku uvantu iṟivaṉ nīṇṭa āyuḷum vāḷum aḷittāṉ.
“iṉi icittamiḻ nūlākiya perunāri peruṅkuruvum piṟavum tēvaviruṭi1 nārataṉ ceyta pañcapāratīyam mutalāyuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlkaḷākiya paratam, akattiyam mutalāyuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷumiṟantaṉa. piṉṉar muṟuval, cayantam, kuṇanūl, ceyiṟ ṟiyam eṉpavaṟṟuḷḷum orucār cūttiraṅkaḷ naṭakkiṉṟa attuṇi yallatu mutal naṭu iṟuti kāṇāmiyiṉ aviyum iṟantaṉapōlum. iṟakkavarum peruṅkala mutaliya piṟavumām. ivaṟṟuṭ peruṅkalamāvatu pēriyāḻ; atu kōṭṭiṉataḷavu paṉṉiru cāṇum, vaṇaraḷavu cāṇum, pattaraḷavu paṉṉirucāṇum, ippeṟṟikkēṟṟa āṇikaḷum, tivavum, untiyum peṟṟu, āyiraṅkōl toṭuttiyalvatu; eṉṉi. . . . . . . “tala mutalūḻiyiṟ ṟāṉavar tarukkap, pulamakaḷāḷar purinārap pāyiram, valipeṟat toṭutta vākkami pēriyāḻc, celavu muṟiyellāñ ceykiyiṟ ṟerintu, maṟṟi yāḻuṅ kaṟṟu muṟi piḻiyāṉ” eṉak katiyiṉuḷḷuṅ kūṟiṉārākalāṟ pēriyāḻ mutaliyaṉavum iṟantaṉaveṉak koḷka. “iṉit tēva viruṭiyākiya kuṟumuṉipāṟ kēṭṭa māṇākkar paṉṉiruvaruṭ cikaṇṭiyeṉṉum aruntavamuṉi, iṭic caṅkattu anākulaṉeṉṉum teyva pāṇṭiyaṉ, tērōṭu vicumpu celvōṉ tilōttami eṉṉum teyva makaḷik kaṇṭu tēriṟ kūṭiṉaviṭattuc caṉittāṉit tēvarum muṉivarum cariyā niṟkat tōṉṟiṉamiyiṟ cāra kumaraṉeṉa, appeyar peṟṟa kumāraṉ iciyaṟitaṟkuc ceyta ici nuṇukkamum, pāracamuṉi varil yāmaḷēntirar ceyta intira kāḷiyamum, aṟivaṉār ceyta pañca marapum, ātivāyilār ceyta parata cēṉāpatīyamum, kaṭiccaṅka pāṇṭi yaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiya pāṇṭiyaṉ mativāṇaṉār ceyta mutaṉūlkaḷi luḷḷa vacikkūttiṟku maṟutaliyākiya puṟak kūttiyaṉṟa mativāṇaṉār nāṭakat tamiḻumeṉa ivvintum innāṭakak kāppiyak karuttaṟinta nūlkaḷaṉṟēṉum orupuṭi yoppumi koṇṭu muṭittalik karutiṟṟu ivvuriyeṉak koḷka” eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār uriyāl muṟkālattu viḷaṅkiya ici nāṭaka nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟi aṟikiṉṟōm.
tamiḻukkuriya ēḻicikaḷiṉ peyar kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉpaṉa. ivvicikaḷiṉ ōcik kuvami. vaṇṭu, kiḷi, kutiri, yāṉi, kuyil, tēṉu, āṭu. ivaṟṟiṉ eḻuttāvaṉa: ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, oḷa. icikaḷiṉ cuvikkuvami tēṉ, tayir, ney, ēlam, vāḻik kaṉi, mātuḷaṅkaṉi.
“pāli kuṟiñci marutam cevvaḻi eṉṉum paṇkaḷiyāvatu, antāḷi, maruḷ, kuccari, kuṟaṇṭi, cātāri mutaliya tiṟaṉkaḷiyāvatu iṉṉateṉṟu pāṭuvōr ikkālattu ettuṇiyar uḷar? paṇ eṉpatu paṇ paṇṇiyameṉa iraṇṭākavum, tiṟaṉ eṉpatu tiṟaṉ, tiṟattiṟaṉ eṉa iraṇṭākavum pakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aviyellām ippoḻutu rākam eṉṉum peyarip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cempālip paṇṇeṉṟu muṉṉōr kūṟiyatu ikkālattuc caṅkarāparaṇap peyarōṭu vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. cātāri eṉṟu akkālattiṟ collappaṭṭatu nīlāmpari eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu paḻiya paṇṇun tiṟaṉun tam peyariyiḻantu ēḻiciyiṉ peyarkaḷākiya kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉṟiruntaṉa māṟic caṭcam mutal niṭātam variyiluḷḷa camak kirutap peyarkaḷip peṟṟu ca,ri,ka,ma,pa,ta,ni eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.” -tamiḻ varalāṟu
“kuṟavañci paḷḷu cintu mutaliya piṟkālap pirapantaṅkaḷum iciyic cērntaṉavē. icittamiḻ nūlkaḷ ivvaḷavu iruntaṉa veṉpataṉāl akkālattil irunta iciyamippiṉ viri vuṇarappaṭum. cilappatikāram mutaliyavaṟṟāl iciyippaṟṟit terintavikaḷiṟ cila kūṟuvēṉ.
1. icivaki
iciyil paṇkaḷeṉṟum tiṟaṅkaḷeṉṟum iruvaki uṇṭu. paṇkaḷ 7 narampukaḷuṅ koṇṭaṉa. narampu eṉpatu iṅkē svaram. ēḻu svaramum koṇṭami campūraṇa rākam. atuvē paṇṇām, vaṭamoḻi yil mēḷakarttā veṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatum aḵtē. ēḻu svaraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil ṣaṭjam, riṣapam, kāntāram, matyamam, pañcamam, tivatam, niṣātam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. avaṟṟiyē, tamiḻil kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. yāvarukkum iyalpāṉa kural ṣaṭjam ākum. ataṉik kuraleṉṟē vaḻaṅkiya peyaramiti viyakkaṟpālatu. ēḻu svaraṅkaḷukkum ca,ri,ka,ma,pa,ta,ni eṉṟu ippoḻutu payilappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷip pōlavē tamiḻ muṟiyil ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, oḷa eṉṟa ēḻu neṭiliyum svaraṅkaḷukku eḻuttuk kaḷākak koṇṭu payiṉṟaṉar.
inta ēḻu narampukaḷum niṟinta rākam paṇṇeṉṟu muṉṉarē coṉṉēṉ. jaṉakarākameṉpatum atuvē. appaṇkaḷiliruntu tiṟaṅkaḷ piṟakkum. avi ippōtu jaṉyarākaṅka ḷeṉṟu vaḻaṅkap paṭum. ‘nirinaram piṟṟē tiṟameṉap paṭumē’ eṉṟa tivākarac cūttirattāl paṇkaḷ, tiṟaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇam viḷaṅkum. paṇkaḷukku iṉamākat tiṟaṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭum. yāppilakkaṇattiṟ “paṇṇun tiṟamum pōṟ pāvu miṉamumāy, vaṇṇa vikaṟpa vakiyamiyāl-paṇṇiṉ, tiṟam viḷarik killatupōṟ ceppalakava, licimaruṭku milliyiṉam” eṉṟu kāṇappaṭum ilakkaṇattil itu viḷakkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
ippaṭip piṟakkum paṇṇun tiṟamumām ici vakikaḷ oruvaḻi yiṟ ṟokukkappaṭṭu 11,991 eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṇkaḷ palavakip paṭum. kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉap perumpaṇkaḷ aintu. ivikaḷiṉ vakiyākum paṇkaḷ pala.ivikaḷuḷ pakaṟpaṇkaḷ mutaliyaviyum, avvappoḻutiṟ kaminta paṇkaḷum yāmaṅkaḷuk kuriyaṉavum, cuvikaṭ kuriyaṉavum vaṇaṅkutaṟ kuriyaṉavu meṉap palavaki uṇṭu. puṟanīrmi mutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭu paṇkaḷ pakaṟpaṇkaḷeṉappaṭum. takkarāka mutaliya oṉpatu paṇkaḷum irāppaṇkaḷeṉpar. cevvaḻi mutal mūṉṟum potup paṇkaḷām. kālikkuriya paṇ marutam. mālik kuriyatu cevvaḻi eṉpārumuḷar. intap paṇkaḷāl icivallōrkaḷ cila kuṟippiṉi aṟivippatuṇṭu. oruvar tam naṇpaṉi māliyil varavēṇṭi mālip paṇṇip pāṭiṉāreṉṟu ōriṭattil terivikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
“iraṅkaṟpaṇ viḷari. atip pāṭip piṟarpāl irakkam uṇṭākkiṉārkaḷ. paramacivaṉāl kiliyiṉ kīḻ amiḻttappaṭṭa irāvaṇaṉ avarukku irakkamuṇṭāka viḷariyip pāṭiṉāṉ eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa “virāy malarp pūṅkuḻalipākaṉ makiḻviṉōṅkum veḷḷimalik kīḻk kiṭantu viḷari pāṭum, irāvaṇaṉār” eṉṟu oru kavi pāṭiyuḷḷār. ic ceyyuṭ pakuti viḷari iraṅkaṟpaṇ eṉpati naṉku terivippatu kāṇka. pāṭuṅkāl ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷōṭu pāṭavēṇṭum eṉṟa viṣayaṅkaḷi mika virivāka ici nūlkaḷ kūṟum. kuṟṟam cila vakippaṭum. ik kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pāṭuvōr pāl iruttal kūṭāveṉa vilakkiyum nūlkaḷ kūṟum. -ṭākṭar u.vē.cāminātayyar - makā makōpāttiyāyar
āriyappirāmaṇaruṭiya ātikkattāl tamiḻarkkuriya paṇkaḷ nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟap payilvāraṟṟu maṅkip pōkavē vaṭatici yirākaṅkaḷ vaḷarntōṅki eṅkum vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭaṉa. paḻiya paṇkaḷ ōynta kālam iṉṉateṉṟu kuṟippiṭal acāttiyamāyiṉum, māṇikkavācakar kālattē avi maṅkiyirukkat tuvaṅkiyirukkalāmeṉṟum, tiru vicippā kālamākiya kiṟittāptam 10 allatu 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tēvatā stōttiraṅkaḷukku māttiram arhamāka otuṅkiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum eṇṇutal paḻutākātu. aḵtu eppaṭi āyiṉumākuka. ippōtu nam nāṭṭil paravum pūpāḷam, māḷavi, paṅkāḷam, kāmpōti, kalyāṇi, kāntāri mutaliya irākaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ peyarālēyē taṅkaḷ piṟappiṭam uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ik kālattu vaḻaṅkum maṟṟa irākaṅkaḷum appaṭiyē puṟanāṭṭu varameṉṟu nirūpittal aritaṉṟu. iṅṅaṉam icikaḷ māṟavē, putiya vaṭatēcattu irākaṅkaḷil aminta ciṟcila patam kīrttaṉi ivaṟṟuṭ kalakkak kalakkap paḻiya āṭṭam pāṭṭu vaṭivāyt tamiḻ nāṭaka nūlkaḷum naṭappavāyiṉa. - nāṭakat tamiḻ ārāycci -ilakkumi
1intap paṇkaḷum kūttukaḷum evvakiyiṉaveṉṟu oruvarālum ippoḻutu kūṟamuṭiyātu. avikaḷiṉ iṭatti intu āriya irākaṅkaḷum nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷum eṭuttukkoṇṭaṉa. -tamiḻ ārāycci
2. icikkaruvikaḷ
icikkaruvikaḷ aintu. avi tōṟkaruvi, tuḷikkaruvi, narampukkaruvi, kañcakkaruvi, miṭaṟṟukkaruvi eṉpaṉa. tōṟkaruvikaḷāvaṉa pēriki, paṭakam, iṭakki, uṭukki, mattaḷam, calliki, karaṭiki, timili, kuṭamuḻā, takki, kaṇappaṟi, tamarukam, taṇṇumi, taṭāri, antari, muḻavu, cantiravaḷiyam, monti, muracu, kaṇviṭu tūmpu, nicāḷam, tuṭumi, ciṟupaṟi, aṭakkam, takuṇiccam, viralēṟu, pākam, upāṅkam, nāḻikippaṟi, tuṭi perumpaṟi eṉpaṉa.
mattaḷam-mattu ōcip peyar; iciyiṉālākiya karuvikaṭ kellām taḷamātalāl mattaḷam eṉṉum peyarāyiṟṟu. calliki yeṉpatu calleṉṟa ōciyuṭittātalāṟ peṟṟa peyar. āvañci yeṉiṉum, kuṭakki yeṉiṉum, iṭakki yeṉiṉum okkum; ataṟku āviṉuṭiya vañcittōlip pōrttalāl vañciyeṉṟu peya rāyiṟṟu; kuṭukkiyāka vaḷittalāṟ kuṭukki yeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu; viṉikkiriyikaḷ iṭakkiyāṟ ceytaliṉ iṭakki yeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu; karaṭi kattiṉāṟpōlum ōciyuṭittātalāṟ karaṭiki yeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. -aṭiyārkku nallār.
“takki taṇṇumi tāḷam vīṇi
takuṇic caṅkiṇi callari
kokkari kuṭamuḻa viṉō ṭici
kūṭippāṭiniṉ ṟāṭuvīr.” cuntarar-tē.
tuḷikkaruvi-kuḻal. narampukkaruvikaḷ-ātiyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ mutaliya nālvaki yāḻkaḷumām. ivaṟṟuḷ ātiyāḻukku āyiram narampum, makarayāḻukkup pattoṉpatum, cakōṭayāḻukkup patiṉāṉkum, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukku ēḻumām. vīṇi vaṭaticiyāriyar teṉṉāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu vantatōr icikkaruvi. yāḻvakiyil oṉṟēṉum ikkālattiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli. yāḻukku vēṇṭiya māṭṭu narampukaḷi nantaṉār vamicattār koṭuppār eṉṟu periya purāṇattiṟ collappaṭukiṉṟatu. yāḻ kiviralkaḷiṉāl taṭavi olikkappeṟum.
11. nāṭakattamiḻ
“nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum-pāṭalcāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam” eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiram akkālam nāṭaka nūlkaḷ mikku vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟatu. cilappatikāram araṅkēṟṟu kātiyāṉum ataṟku aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiya uriyāṉum paḻiya ici nāṭakaṅkaḷik kuṟitta pala ariya ceytikaḷ aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. nāṭakat tamiḻ aḻinili eytiya ikkālattup paṇṭiya nāṭakac ciṟappiṉi muṟṟa uṇarttum nūlkaḷ kiṭittila.
“nāṭaka vaḻakkāvatu, cuvipaṭa varuvaṉavellām ōriṭattu vantaṉavākat tokuttuk kūṟutal; aḵtāvatu celvattāṉum, kulat tāṉum, oḻukkattāṉum, aṉpiṉāṉum otta iruvarāyt tamariṉ nīṅkit taṉiyiṭattu etirppaṭṭār eṉavum, avvaḻik koṭuppōrum aṭuppōrumiṉṟi vēṭki mikutiyāṟ puṇarntāreṉavum, piṉṉum avarkaḷ kaḷavoḻukkam naṭatti ilakkaṇa vakiyāṉ varinteytiṉār eṉavum piṟavum in nikaraṉavākic cuvippaṭa varuvaṉavellām oruṅku vantaṉavākak kūṟutal.” -iḷampūraṇavaṭikaḷ
“tamiḻ nāṭakam mutaliluṇṭāṉatu mata viṭayamākavēyeṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. atu kaṭavuḷar tiruviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil āṭal pāṭalka ḷiraṇṭiyuñ cēra nikaḻttuvatiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. cila kālattiṉ piṉṉark kati naṭiyāṉa maṉappāṭaṅkaḷum uṭaṉ kūṭiṉa; ataṉmēl mutaliṟ pāṭalāyuḷḷa campāṣaṇikaḷum avaṟṟuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟpāṭu nāṭakat tamiḻ vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟa piriviṉatāki aracarkaḷāṉum ēṉiyōrāṉum ātarittu vaḷarkkappaṭṭatu. ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉātal allākkāl ataṉiṉuñ caṟṟu muṟkālatti ṉātal nāṭakat tamiḻ uyarnili peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. nāmuṇarntuḷa paḻamiyāṉa nāṭakat tamiḻnūlkaḷ aṉittum akkālattē niṉṟu nilaviṉavātali ṉeṉka. ākavē atu kuṟṟaṅ kuṟivu illātu uṇṭāṉa toḻileṉṟē ātiyil matikkappaṭṭatu. iṉi nāṭakat tamiḻ,uyarnili vīḻnili aḻinili eṉṟa mūṉṟu vaki niliyum aṭintu urukkulintu pōṉa piṉṉarē yām ataṉik kaṇṇuṟukiṉṟaṉam.” -nāṭakaviyal
“nāṭakameṉap potuppeyar kūṟat takunta poḻutu pōkki liruntutāṉ palvēṟu rūpamāṉa ellā ‘naṟkali’ (Fine arts)kaḷum piṟap paṉavāyiṉum, pāṭṭum paṇṇum āṭṭamumākiya mūṉṟum ataṉiṉiṉ ṟum nērē uṟpavikkiṉṟaṉa. kāla viḷampaṉattālum nākarikat tērcci yālum pāvaṉiyil naṭattik kāṭṭum toḻilkaḷi naṉku viḷakkattakka vacaṉaṅkaḷik kātukkiṉimi peṟak kōttu moḻitalum, kaṇṇuk kiṉimi peṟak karacaraṇāti avayavaṅkaḷālum mukakkuṟikaḷālum api nayittuk kāṭṭalum, vaḻakkattil varavē, pāṭṭum ataṉuṭaṉ puṇarnta paṇṇum aviyiraṇṭuṭaṉ kalanta āṭṭamum taṉittaṉi piṟappaṉa vāyiṉa. pāṭṭiṉ cīr, ici, taḷi , aṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum appāṭal viḷaṅkum navaracaṅkaḷiyum māttiram kavaṉittu apivirutti paṇṇi ṉāl atu vittuvatcaṉa pūcitamāṉa kāviyam eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟṟu niṟkum. appaṭiyē paṇṇi māttiram vicēṭamākak kavaṉittuc coṟkōttal caṅkītameṉat taṉittu vaḷarum. āṭṭamum avvāṟē cila cātiyāruḷḷum taṉippaṭat taḻittatēṉum muṉṉaviyiraṇṭuṅ kalantu vaḻaṅkalē perumpāṉmi. aṅṅaṉaṅ kalantu vaḻaṅkumpōtu nāṭaka meṉa oru ciṟappup peyar kūṟutal vaḻakkam.
“namatu tamiḻnāṭṭilum immuṟiyē immūṉṟu naṟkalikaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭu ēṟpaṭṭu vantami paṟṟiyē tamiḻi, iyal,ici , nāṭakam eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakiyāka muṉṉōr vakuttaṉar. immūṉṟum nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟa vēṟu vēṟu urukkoṇṭu māṟupāṭu aṭintuḷḷaṉa veṉpatu yāvarukkun terinta ceyti. kāviyam aṭintirukkum oru ciṟu vikaṟpam pattuppāṭṭiyāvatu kalittokiyiyāvatu ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkum kampar irāmāyaṇattōṭō pirapuliṅka līliyōṭō oppiṭuṅkāl viḷaṅkātirātu. avvāṟē ippōtu pōkkiyamāka vaḻaṅkum tēvārap paṇṇōṭu tōṭi piravi eṉṉum irākaṅkaḷil ākkappaṭṭuḷḷa muttut tāṇṭavar kīrttaṉaṅkaḷi oppiṭuṅkāl, icittamiḻ aṭintirukkum vēṟṟumi teriyalākum. muṉṉōr kēṭṭu makiḻnta icikaḷum, avaṟṟit tutti vāttiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiya icikkaruvikaḷum perumpālum māyntupōyiṉa veṉṟē colla lām.‘iṉṉici vīṇiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl’ eṉṟa tiruvācakat tālēyē ikkālattil vaḻaṅkum vīṇiyaṉṟu, cilappatikāram mutaliya paḻiya nūlkaḷil namatu maṉattukku eḷitil vārāvaṇṇam varuṇikkap paṭṭirukkum yāḻeṉpatu veḷippaṭiyākum. yāḻ, tirāviṭarkkuriya tum, vīṇi, vaṭatici āriyar teṉticikkuk koṇṭuvanta putiyatōr icikkaruviyumām. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ icikaḷum māṇṭupōṉa vaṇṇamē tamiḻ nāṭakaṅkaḷum palavāṟu vēṟṟumippaṭṭu aṉāti vaṭivam iṉṉateṉṟu ūkittal arumiyāmpaṭi iṟantupōyiṉatākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
“ippōtu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkivarum nāṭakaṅkaḷ yāvum aru ṇācalak kavirāyar iyaṟṟiya irāma nāṭakattip piṉṟoṭarntaṉavā yiruppatiṉālum kīrttaṉic cāyi paḻiya nūlkaḷil kāṇāmiyālum pūrvīka nāṭakameṉṟu niccayikkattakkatu yātoṉṟum ituvari veḷippaṭāmiyālum, tamiḻmoḻikkē yurittāṉa yātēṉum nāṭakam iruntatu tāṉum uṇṭō eṉṟu cilar aiyuṟal iyalpēyām. āṉāl, atippōlavē cantēkippataṟku mutaṟ kāraṇam nāṭakameṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyilum āṅkilattilum nām kaṇṭu paḻakum nāṭakattukkē peyarāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum mayakkameṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu...1 nāṭakameṉṉum vaṭamoḻikkuc cariyāṉa teṉmoḻi kūttu eṉpatē. tivākarattiṉpaṭi kūttiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷ “naṭamē nāṭakaṅ kaṇṇu ṇaṭṭam paṭika māṭa ṟāṇṭavam parata-māluta ṟūṅkaḷ vāṇi kuravi yiyal nivaya niruttam” mutaliyaṉavām. ātalāl tamiḻar kūttu nāṭaka meṉṟu ciṟappittuk koṇṭaviyaṉittum āṭalōṭamintavi yāka iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu koḷḷutal tavaṟākātu. mēḷatāḷaṅ kaḷukkiciyumāṟu yātēṉumoru pāviṉattila katiyi yamittu antak katiyip pāṭalōṭōti apinayittalē ātikālat tamiḻ nāṭaka rītiyeṉṟu pala kāraṇaṅkaḷālumeṇṇutaṟ kiṭamuṇṭu. avvita āṭal vikaṟpaṅkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉa eṉpataṟkup paḻiya kāviyaṅkaḷuṭ kāṇum māyōṉāṭal, civaṉāṭal, kumaraṉāṭal, intirāṇiyāṭal, turkki yāṭal, cattamātarāṭal, kāmaṉāṭal, vēlaṉāṭal, veṟiyāṭal, picācuk kikkūttu, kuravikkūttu eṉpaṉa ātiyāṉa moḻikaḷē cāṉṟāka niṟkum, ‘malaṭi mintaṉukku mālicūṭṭal pōla’ illāta nāṭakat tukku ittaṉi peyar vakittal iyalpaṉṟu. mēlum mēṟkūṟiya kūttu vakikaḷ paṟpala kāraṇac cērkkiyāl tamiḻnāṭṭil māyntupōyirup piṉum aḻintavikaṭkellām apayap piratāṉaṅ koṭuttu ātarikkum ellipōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟa maliyāḷattuḷ pala kirāmāntaraṅkaḷil iṉṉum vaḻaṅkukiṟa aivarkaḷi, paricikaḷi, ōṭṭat tuḷḷal mutalāṉa palavēṟu āṭaṟ katikaḷik kāṇpavarkkut tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatum pūrvattil vaḻaṅki vanta nāṭaka vikaṟpaṅkaḷ ittaṉmiyaṉaveṉṟu ūkittal oruvāṟu kūṭiyatākavēyirukkiṉṟatu. āṭṭakkatiyeṉpatu maliyāḷattil nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ peyarākavumirukkiṉṟatu. kuṟattippāṭṭu, uḻattippāṭṭu mutaliya paḻiya pirapanta rītiyāyp pāṭiyāṭum pāvikaṟpaṅkaḷ iṉṉum pala tamiḻnāṭṭilum villaṭip pāṭṭu. uṭukkup pāṭṭu mutaliyaṉa āṭṭattōṭu kati virittalukkē urittāṉavi; noṇṭic cintu mutaliya pala vikaṟpaṅkaḷum paṟpala tāḻicikaḷum āṭalukku mika ēṟṟaṉa. ātalāl paḻiya nāṭakaṅkaḷ villuppāṭṭu tuḷḷaṟpāṭṭu mutaliyavipōla oruvar taṉiyāyēṉum, kummippāṭṭu paricikaḷi mutaliyaṉapōlap palarkūṭiyēṉum oru katiyit tāḷa mēḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pāṭi apinayittuk kūṟum oruvakip pāṭṭāka irun tirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu niruṇayikkavēṇṭum.
“appaṭiyāṉāl tamiḻnāṭakaṅkaḷ yātoṉṟum ippōtu kāṇak kiṭiyāmikku vicēṭa kāraṇan tēṭavēṇṭiyataṟ kavaciya milli.
“paṭittaṟintu viyattaliyē payaṉākakkoṇṭa paḻiya iyaṟ ṟamiḻk kāviyaṅkaḷukkuḷ ikkālam namakkeṭṭiṉavi ettaṉi, irunta viṭamē teriyātu iṟantu pōṉavi ettaṉi eṉak kaṇakkiṭuvō mākil ippōtu cīvikkiṟa maṉitarkaḷiyum iṟantōriyum ‘eṇṇik koṇṭaṟṟu’ eṉavaṉṟō matikkattakkatākavirukkiṉṟatu. paṭitta māttirattil oṉpāṉ cuvi tarattakka iyaṟṟamiḻiṉatu kati iṅṅaṉa māyiṉ, āṭik kaṇṭāloḻiya iṉpam payavāta nāṭakappāṭṭukkaḷ kāla viḷampaṉattil aḻintupōṉatu oru viyappā? matāpimāṉattāl āḻvār aruḷic ceyta tiruvāy moḻiyum mūvar tēvāraṅkaḷum oru vāṟu avaṟṟiṉ paṇṇum piṟkālattār aṟiyumpaṭi nilinillā viṭil paḻiya icit tamiḻum ivvāṟē uṇṭāyiruntatō illiyō eṉṉum cantēkattukkaṉṟō iṭantaruvatāka niṟkum? cuttavittiyā viṣayamāṉa apimāṉam nīṅkik kēvalam camayac caccaravukaḷāl mūṭap paṭṭa iṭikkālattil, iyaṟṟamiḻk kāviyat talimipeṟṟa cintā maṇi cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷē tappiṉatu tālippākkiyamāy irukkumāṉāl, kāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷākiya icittamiḻa nūlkaḷum, āṭṭappāṭṭāṉa nāṭaka nūlkaḷum maṇṇuṟa māṇṭu pōṉami caṟṟumē oru āccariyamā? icit tamiḻum nāṭakat tamiḻum tammuḷ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉavāka ekkālamumirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu eḷitiṟṟuṇiyat takkatē.
“tuvakkattil putiyaṉa ciṟupāṉmiyum paḻiya cintu tāḻici mutaliyaṉa mikutiyākavumiruntirukkavēṇṭum. kālañ cellac cellap paḻiyaṉa kuṟintum putiya kīrttaṉaṅkaḷ mikuntum vantaṉa. aruṇācalak kavirāyar irāma nāṭakattilum kuṟṟālak kuṟavañci mutaliya paḻimi paṟṟiya nāṭakaviyalkaḷilum, ivi iraṇṭu vakippaṭṭa āṭṭap pāṭṭukaḷ kalantu kiṭappaṉa kāṇalākum. arumi perumikaḷiṟ ciṟanta aruṇācalak kavirāyar tamatu oppaṟṟa nāṭakam amitta piṉṉar ataṉiyē vāyppāṭamākak koṇṭupiṟkālattavar paṟpala nāṭakañ ceytaṉar; avaṟṟuḷḷum paḻiya cintu mutaliyavi irāvaṇap pōril vipīṣaṇaṉ pōla, iṉṉum iṟavāmal āṅkāṅkut talikāṭṭi niṟṟal pulappaṭakkūṭum. ivvāṟu paḻiya nāṭakap pāṭṭiṉiyalpu muṟṟum māṟutalaṭintu pōyiṉum, oru niliyil paḻiya tamiḻ nāṭakaviyalpu caṟṟum māṟupaṭātu niṉṟu koṇṭatu. ikkālattu nāṭakaṅkaḷ enta enta vaṭivamāka iruntapōtilum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum āṭṭamiṉṟi naṭantēṟutal kūṭiyataṉṟu. ivvāṭṭamē muṟkūṟiyapaṭi tamiḻkkūttiṟ kum ēṉiya nāṭakaṅkaḷukkum uḷḷa talimiyāṉa vēṟṟumi. paṇ, pā, araṅku mutaliya yāvum vēṟṟumippaṭum. tamiḻk kūttukkuriya āṭal oṉṟum itu varikkum pētappaṭavilli.” -nāṭakat tamiḻ ārāycci.
“ivvāṟu tōṉṟi vaḷarnta nāṭakattamiḻ vīḻniliyaṭiyap pukuntatu. ataṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? oḻukkanili vakukkap pukunta āriyarum ciṉarum nāṭakak kāṭciyāṟ kāmamē aṟiviṉum mikap perukukiṉṟa teṉṉum pōlik koḷkiyuṭiyarāyt tam nūlkaḷiṟ kaṭiyappaṭupa vaṟṟuḷ nāṭakattiyuñ cērttuk kūṟiṉar.1 akkālattirunta aracarkkut turppōtaṉi ceytu nāṭakat tamiḻit taliyeḻavoṭṭātu aṭakki vantaṉar. oḷaviyārum tiruvaḷḷuvarum oruṅkē pukaḻnta illaṟa vāḻkkiyit tīviṉiyaccattiṉpāṟpaṭuttik kūṟuñ ciṉarkaḷ nāṭakat tamiḻnūlik kaṭintatu ōr āccariyamaṉṟu. ivvaḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭṭuk kiṭiyil nāṭakattamiḻ evvāṟu taliyeṭuttu ōṅkap pōkiṉṟatu. ataṉmēl iyaṟṟamiḻk kāppiyaṅkaḷ nāṭeṅkum malintu ciṟantu nāṭakat tamiḻi vaḷaravoṭṭātu taṭuppaṉavāyiṉa. ivvāṟu nāṭakat tamiḻukku nāṟpuṟattiṉun taṭikaḷamikkap paṭṭamiyiṉ aḵtu aḻinili eytat talippaṭṭatu. paṇṭitarāṉōr kaṭintu nāṭakat tamiḻik kiviṭavē atu pāmarar kiyakappaṭṭu iḻivaṭintu teruk kūttaḷavilē niṟkiṉṟatu.” -tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu.
cintāmaṇi cūḷāmaṇi mutaliya kāmaccuvi malinta ciṉa kāppiyaṅ kaḷi nōkkumiṭattuc ciṉarkaḷ nāṭakat tamiḻi vaḷaravoṭṭātu taṭuttārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟa iṭamilli.
12. tamiḻ ilakkaṇattiṉ ciṟappiyalpu
vaṭamoḻi tamiḻ eṉṉum irumoḻippulamiyum oruṅkē nirampiya civañāṉa cuvāmikaḷ “tamiḻ moḻip puṇarccikkaṭpaṭum kuṟiyīṭukaḷum, viṉikkuṟippu viṉittoki mutaliya collilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi mutaliya coṟpākupāṭukaḷum, kuṟiñci veṭci mutaliya tiṇippākupāṭukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum iṉṉōraṉṉavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟap paṭā” eṉṟum, “īṇṭuk kūṟum poruṭ pākupāṭukaḷ potuvākātu tamiḻiṟkē ciṟantu vēṟoṉṟāṟ peṟappaṭāmiyiṉ ipporuḷ paṟṟi varum paripāṭal, kali, akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, naṟṟiṇi, kuṟuntoki, āṟṟuppaṭi, patiṟṟuppattu mutaliya ceyyuḷ ārāyappukuntāṟku ipporuṭpākupāṭu uṇarākkāl kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurīip pōla iṭarppaṭuvar”eṉṟum, “cōtiṭam mutaliya piṟakalikaḷel lām āriyattiṉum tamiḻiṉum, ēṉimoḻikaḷiṉum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi oppa nikaḻtaliṉ, avaṟṟi vēṟu vitikka vēṇṭāmiyāṉum, iyalici nāṭakameṉṉum mūṉṟum tamiḻ nilattuc ciṟitu vēṟupāṭuṭimiyiṉ avaṟṟi vitikka vēṇṭutalāṉum atu paṟṟi akattiyattuḷ mūṉṟumē yeṭuttōtiṉār” eṉṟum tolkāppiyac cūttira viruttiyiṉkaṇ āṅkāṅku urittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
“tamiḻ moḻiyil ellāp poruḷkaḷum iraṇṭu tiṇikaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭuḷa. pakuttaṟivuṭiya uyirkaḷi uyartiṇiyākap pakuttu uyartiṇiyil āṇukku āṇpālum, peṇṇukkup peṇpālum, palarukkup palarpālum, pakuttaṟivaṟṟa vilaṅku, paṟavi, nīrvāḻvaṉa mutaliya uyirkaḷiyum, maram, koṭi, ceṭi, paṟavi nīrvāḻvaṉa mutaliya uyir kaḷiyum aḵṟiṇiyākap pakuttu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟukku oṉṟaṉpālum, palavaṟṟukkup palaviṉpālum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻil vilaṅku, paṟavi, mīṉkaḷiṉ āṇ peṇkaḷukkum vevvēṟu peyar kūṟappaṭiṉum, avikaḷ pakuttaṟivaṟṟa tāḻnta cātiyiṟ paṭṭaṉa vātalāl aḵṟiṇi yākavē kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṅkilam mutaliya moḻikaḷil cila aḵṟiṇi uyirkaḷukkum, poruḷkaḷukkum āṇpāl peṇpāl kūṟiṉum aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭṭa oṉṟiyē pala pālkaḷil kūṟuvatum, uyartiṇi āṇpālukkup peṇpāṟ peyarum, peṇpālukku āṇpāl oṉṟaṉpāl peyarum muṟi piṟaḻntu kūṟuvatum illi.
vaṭamoḻiyilō pāl varampiṉṟip palavāṟu kūṟuvatuṇṭu. avi varumāṟu:-
1. pāryā - peṇpāl (orumi)
2. tārā - āṇpāl (eṉṟum paṉmi)
3. kaḷatram - oṉṟaṉpāl
1. kalliṉ peyar
1. pāṣāṇa - āṇpāl
2. cilā - peṇpāl
3. upalam - oṉṟaṉpāl
ātalāl tamiḻ pāltiṇiyil iṉitu.
“oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉolikaḷi varivaṭiviṟ kāṇpippataṟkuk karuviyāyuḷḷatu eḻuttu. aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻmoḻiyuḷ oliyum, ataṟkuriya eḻuttum, oṟṟumi peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cila moḻikaḷip pōl avi oṉṟukkoṉṟu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉavalla.
vaṭamoḻiyil
prahma eṉṟeḻutip pramha eṉṟolikkiṉṟaṉar.
cihma eṉṟeḻutip cimha eṉṟolikkiṉṟaṉar.
vahani eṉṟeḻuti vanhi eṉṟolikkiṉṟaṉar.
vijñāṉam eṉṟeḻuti vikñāṉam eṉṟolikkiṉṟaṉar.
2. viṉit teḷivu
“viṉiccol oruvaṉ orutti oṉṟaṉ ceyalkaḷic kuṟikkuñ col. tamiḻmoḻiyil viṉikaḷ avaṟṟit teḷivupeṟak kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. piṟa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa viṉiccoṟkaḷ āṇ peṇ oṉṟaṉ pālkaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭṭi viḷakkukiṉṟila.
āṅkilam vaṭamoḻi
celkiṉṟāṉ (He) goes (sa) kaccati
celkiṉṟāṉ (She) goes (sā) kaccati
celkiṉṟatu (It) goes (tat) kaccati
āṅkilam ilattīṉ
nēcikkiṟāṉ (He) loves (llle) amat
nēcikkiṟāḷ (She) loves (llla) amat
nēcikkiṟatu (It) loves (lllud) amat
3. coṟṟoṭaraḻaku
“coṟṟoṭar -vākkiyam. atu eḻuvāy payaṉili. ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉpaṉa kūṭi oru karutti viḷakkuvatu. avaṟṟōṭu avaṟṟiṉ uriccoṟkaḷum, iṭippiṟa varalāy varum vākkiyaṅkaḷum cērntu kuḻuc coṟṟoṭarāyum varum. peyar viṉikaḷiṉ iyali uriccoṟ kaḷāṉum, pala peyarkaḷi iṭiccoṟkaḷāṉum, palaviṉikaḷi eccattāṉum, peyarkkum viṉikkum uḷḷa campantatti vēṟṟumi yurupāṉum puṇarttit tamiḻil viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṅkilattilum, tamiḻ pōlavē coṟṟoṭa ruḷatu.
“vaṭamoḻi, ilattīṉ, kirīkku mutaliya moḻikaḷil aṅṅaṉam aḻaku peṟātu vicēṭaṇaṅkaḷukkum avi taḻuvum peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi pālkaḷiyēṟṟal, iṭiccol iṉṟik kūṟal mutaliya aḻakiṉmiyuḷḷatu.
utāraṇam
(1) “kariya periya kuyili vañcakamuṭiya kākkiyāka eṇṇi ṉāṉ.
kruṣṇam prahantam vañcaki nam kākam pēṉō kariyati periyati kuyili vañcakamuṭiyatik kākkiyi eṇṇiṉāṉ.
(2) maturiyilirukkum irāmaṉ makaṉ pulavaṉākiya cumukaṉāṉa ilakkumaṇaṉāl itu eḻutappaṭṭatu.
maturāvācinā rāma puttirēṇa paṇṭitēna cumukēna laṣmaṇēna iyam likyatē.
maturiyil vacippavaṉāl irāmaṉ puttiraṉāl cumukaṉāl ilaṭcumaṇaṉāl itu eḻutappaṭṭatu.’
(moḻinūl-mākaṟal kārttikēya mutaliyār)
4. ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ
akattiyar, tamiḻukkuc1 ciṟṟakattiyam pērakattiyam eṉṉum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ ceytāreṉpa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum ikkālattuk kiṭikkavilli. citaṟuṇṭa cūttiraṅkaḷeṉac cilavaṟṟit tokuttu, “pērakattiyat tiraṭṭu” oṉṟu accaṭittirukkiṉṟaṉar. accūttiraṅkaḷiṉ coṉṉaṭiyum, poruṇaṭiyum nōkkum iḷañ ciṟārum avi paṇṭit tamiḻaṉṟu eṉṟu tuṇintu koḷvar. akattiyattukku muṉ kumaram eṉṉum ōr ilakkaṇam iruntatākak kūṟupa. pērāṟṟu neṭuntuṟiyaṉ perunūlkaḷum iṭikkaḻic ceṅkōṭaṉ iyaṉūl mutaliya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum iruntu maṟintu pōṉatākac ceṅkōṉ tariccelavu eṉṉum nūlāl aṟikiṉṟōm. tolkāppiyattiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum ‘eṉpa’, “eṉmaṉār pulavar’, ‘iyal puṇarntōr moḻipa’ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷāl tol kāppiyar kālattum muṉpum pala ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa veṉpatu teḷivu.
yāpparuṅkala virutti yāpparuṅkalakkāriki (paḻiya) uri mutaliyavaṟṟil ikkālattuk kiṭiyāta pala ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mēṟkōḷkaḷāka vantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
ikkālam vaḻaṅkum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ, tolkāppiyam, vīracōḻi yam, yāpparuṅkalakkāriki, taṇṭiyalaṅkāram, māṟaṉalaṅkāram, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam, ilakkaṇakkottu, nēminātam, naṉṉūl mutaliyaṉa.
piṟa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum piṟavumāyuḷḷa nūlkaḷ muṉṉiruntavaṟṟiliruntu virittu eḻutap peṟum. tamiḻ nūlkaḷō muṉṉiruntavaṟṟic curukki eḻutappaṭuvaṉavākum. utāraṇattukkut tolkāppiyam, naṉṉūl, ilakkaṇac curukkam mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka.
13. caṅkam
“talic caṅkam iṭic caṅkam kaṭic caṅkameṉa mūvakippaṭṭa caṅkam irīiyiṉār pāṇṭiyarkaḷ, avaruḷ taliccaṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārum, tiripurameritta viricaṭik kaṭavuḷuṅ, kuṉṟa meṟinta kumaravēḷum, murañciyūr muṭinākarāyarum nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉumeṉa it toṭakkattār aiññūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭa nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉā reṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa ettaṉiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāriyum, mutukurukum, kaḷariyāviriyu meṉa ittoṭakkattaṉa. avar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅka mirīiyiṉār kāyciṉa vaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkō ṉīṟāka eṇpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturi yeṉpa. avarukku nūl akattiyam.
“iṉi iṭiccaṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārum tolkāppiya ṉārum, iruntiyūrk karuṅkōḻiyum, mōciyum, veḷḷūrk kāppiya ṉum, ciṟu pāṇṭaraṅkaṉum, tiriyaṉ māṟaṉum, tuvarik kōṉum, kīrantiyumeṉa it toṭakkattār aimpat toṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭa mūvāyirat teḻunūṟṟuvar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa kaliyum, kurukum veṇṭāḷiyum viyāḻamāliyakavalu meṉa ittoṭakkattaṉa eṉpa. avarkku nūl akattiyamun tolkāp piyamum, māpurāṇamum, ici nuṇukkamum, pūta purāṇamumeṉa ivi yeṉpa. avar mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avaric caṅkamīriiyiṉār veṇṭērcceḻiyaṉ mutalāka muṭattiru māṟaṉīṟāka aimpattoṉpatiṉma reṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār aivar pāṇṭiyareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapurat teṉpa. akkālattup pōlum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kaṭal koṇṭatu.
“iṉik kaṭiccaṅka miruntu tamiḻārāyntār ciṟu mētāviyārum, cēntam pūtaṉārum, aṟivuṭiyaraṉārum, peruṅkuṉṟūrk kiḻārum, iḷantirumāṟaṉum, maturiyāciriyar nallantuvaṉārum, marutaṉiya nākaṉārum, kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉāru meṉa it toṭakkattār nāṟpattoṉpatiṉma reṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉ patiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa neṭuntoki nāṉūṟum, kuṟuntoki nāṉūṟum, naṟṟiṉi nāṉūṟum, puṟa nāṉūṟum aiṅkuṟu nūṟum, patiṟṟuppattum, nūṟṟimpatu kaliyum, eḻupatu pari pāṭalum, kūttum, variyum,ciṟṟiciyum, pēriciyumeṉṟu ittoṭak kattaṉa. avarkku nūl akattiyamun tolkāppiyamumeṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu āyiratteṇṇūṟṟimpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu eṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅkamirīiyiṉār kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭup pōn tirunta muṭattiru māṟaṉ mutalāka ukkirap peruvaḻuti yīṟāka nāṟpat toṉpatiṉma reṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār mūvar pāṇṭiya reṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturi yeṉpa.” -iṟiya ṉārakapporuḷuri.
kaḷaviyaluriyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa caṅkap pulavarkaḷ palar caṅkanūṟ ceyyuṭkaḷi iyaṟṟiya pulavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ kaḷaviyaluriyiṟ kāṇappaṭum caṅka varalāṟu ātāramaṟṟa kaṭṭukkati eṉṟu karuta iṭamilli. paṇṭi nāḷiṟ caṅkamiruntu tamiḻ vaḷarttamiyi.
“ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟilic ciṟṟampalattu
meṉ cintiyuḷḷum
uṟivā ṉuyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāynta voṇ
ṭīntamiḻiṉ ṟuṟi” - tirukkōviyār
“ñāṉa campanta ṉuricey caṅkamali centamiḻ” - cam - tē
“tiruvāla vāyil, emmip pavantīrppavar caṅkamiruntu” (periyapurāṇam)
“caṅkat tamiḻmūṉṟun tā” (nalvaḻi)
“..............pāṇṭiyar
pāṭu tamiḻ vaḷartta kūṭaliṉ vaṭāatu” (āciriyamāli)
naṉpāṭṭup pulavaṉāyc caṅkamēṟi naṟkaṉakak
kiḻitarumik karuḷiṉāṉ kāṇ” (tiru.nā.tē.tirupputtūr)
eṉa varum teyvappeṟṟiyāḷar tiruvākkukaḷāṉōrka.
“maturit talapurāṇam kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ mutal maturēcuvara pāṇṭiyaṉīṟāka eḻupattunālu pāṇṭiyarip paṟṟic collukiṉṟatu. ippōtuḷḷa maturiyil aracu purinta pāṇṭiyarkaḷ ivarkaḷtām. kaṭicip pāṇṭiyaṉukku mutal āṇṭa kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ allatu neṭumāṟaṉ ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil iruntavaṉeṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭṭatu. avaṉatu āṭciyil caṅka miruntatāka oru varalāṟuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli. ki.pi.nālām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyilirunta māṇikka vācaka cuvāmikaḷ kālattil caṅkattippaṟṟiya ceyti iṟantakāla nikaḻcci yākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa cilappatikāram, maṇimēkaliyeṉṉum nūlkaḷil caṅkattippaṟṟiya kuṟippuk kāṇappaṭavilli. mūṉṟāñ caṅkattiṉ kaṭicip pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti yeṉpatu kaṉṉaparamparic ceyti. ippāṇṭiyaṉ kālattilēyē tirukkuṟaḷ araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭateṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ippōtu appāṇṭiyaṉ tala purāṇattiṟ collappaṭum 47 pāṇṭiyaruḷ evaṉeṉṟu tīrmāṉikka muṭiyavilli. purāṇameḻuti yavar tamiḻppeyarkaḷic camakkirutattil moḻipeyarttu eḻutiyatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. appaṭi yiruntālum ippāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya kālatti oruvāṟu niccayittal kūṭum. ivaṉ karikāṟcōḻaṉiṉ tantiyākiya iḷañcēṭceṉṉiyiṉ kālattil iruntavaṉeṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ kālam ki.pi.50-90 variyilākum. ākavē, caṅkattiṉ iṟutikkālam mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭileṉṟu tuṇital vēṇṭum. ataṟkup piṉ caṅkamiruntataṟku uṇmic cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkavilli. caṅkam oṭuṅkiyatu mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyilākum. iṉiccaṅkam toṭaṅkiya kālattik kuṟittu ārāyvōm. 196 pāṇṭiyarkaḷ 3900 āṇṭukaḷākac caṅkam naṭattiṉārkaḷeṉṟu muṉ ōriṭattil kūṟap paṭṭatu. or aracaṉukkuriya kālam irupatu eṉa vittuk kaṇakkiṭṭami yiṉ ikkālak kaṇakku ēṟpaṭṭatu. teṉ maturiyilē naṭanta caṅkam 86 aracar ātaraviṉ kīḻ naṭipeṟṟateṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. appaṭi yāyiruntāl 89 pāṇṭiyarukkuriya kāla elli 1780 āṇṭukaḷākiṉṟaṉa. 1780 āṇṭukaḷi 3900 āṇṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum eṭuttuviṭa eñci niṟpatu 2120 āṇṭukaḷākum. itaṉiyē teṉmaturiyik kaṭalkoṇṭa kālameṉak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. calap piraḷayamuṇṭāṉa kālam ki.mu. 2105 eṉa yūtar kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ikkālak kaṇakkut teṉ maturiyik kaṭal koṇṭateṉac collappaṭum kālattōṭu ottiruk kiṉṟatu. kiṟittuvukku muṉ 3900 variyil caṅkat toṭaṅkiyateṉap perumpaṭiyākak kūṟalām. paḻiya tamiḻakam mikavum cīrtiruttam peṟṟirunta teṉpatiyum, atu piṟanāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇikam naṭattiya teṉpatiyum niṉivil vittuk koṇṭāl tamiḻccaṅkam akkālattil naṭi peṟṟirukkak kūṭātu eṉṟu orupōtuṅ karuta iṭam irukkamāṭṭātu. rekōjiṉ1 eṉpār vēta intiyā eṉṉum nūlil kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:- “ūr eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattil ūr eyā eṉṉum vēntaṉāl ki.mu. mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kaṭṭuvikkappaṭṭa paḻiya kaṭṭiṭaṅ kaḷik kiḷaṟip pārttapōtu tamiḻakattil maliyāḷak karikaḷil māttiram vaḷarukiṉṟa tēkka marattuṇṭu oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. vintiyattukku vaṭakkē avvitamāṉa tēkka maram kāṇappaṭuvatilli. atu aiyāyiramāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻarkaḷ evvaḷavu cīrtiruttamaṭin tiruntārkaḷ eṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟatu. ākavē ulaka muḻumiyilum pāṇṭiya vamicamē mikap paḻiyateṉṟum teṉ maturiyil avarkaḷāl niṟuvappaṭṭa caṅkamē ellām caṅkaṅkaḷukkummuṟpaṭṭateṉṟum colvatil āccariyattukkuriyatu oṉṟumilli. paḻiya maturiyiṉ aḻivukkup piṟpāṭu pāṇṭiyarukkut talinakaramāyiruntatu kapāṭapura meṉṟu irāmāyaṇattiṟ paṭikkiṉṟōm. iraṇṭāvatu naṭattiya caṅkam 59 pāṇṭiyariṉ kālam 1180 āṇṭukaḷākum. itu iraṇṭāñ caṅkattiṉ iṟutik kālamum mūṉṟāñ caṅkattiṉ toṭakkamumākiya (2120-1180) ki.mu.940kkuk koṇṭuvantu viṭukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭāvatu talinakaramākiya kapāṭapuram kaṭaṟperukkiṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭateṉpatum avvaḻi vukkup piḻittirunta pāṇṭiyaṉē maṟupaṭiyum kūṭalil caṅkam niṟuviṉā ṉeṉpatum kaṉṉa parampari varalāṟukaḷ. talapurāṇattiṉpaṭi mūṉṟāvatu caṅkatti niṟuviṉavaṉ ukkirapāṇṭiyaṉāvaṉ. māpāratattil collap paṭum pappuravākaṉ ivaṉē. ivaṉ maliyattuvaca pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ pēra ṉeṉṟum maliyattuvacaṉiṉ orē kumāri aruccuṉaṉi maṇantāḷeṉ ṟum māpāratam kūṟukiṉṟatu. talapurāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭum ‘cuntaraṉ’ eṉṉum peyar aruccuṉaṉik kuṟikkum. maliyattuvacaṉi aruccuṉaṉ maṇalūriṟ cantittatāka māpārataṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. kūṭalit talinakarākak koṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ maṇalūriliruntu vantāṉeṉṟu paḻiya tiruviḷi yāṭal purāṇam collukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl kapāṭapurattiṉ aḻivukkup piṟakum kūṭalit talinakarākak koḷvataṟku muṉṉum maṇalūr pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya talinakarāyiruntateṉac collavēṇṭum: allā viṭil maṇalūrum kapāṭapuramum oṉṟu eṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. irāmāyaṇam pāratam eṉṉum iraṇṭu nūlkaḷilum pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya talinakaramākac collappaṭumiṭaṅkaḷ orē iṭattik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. irāmāyaṇattil vāṉmīki cukkīrivaṉ vāṉaravīrarukkuk kūṟiya kūṟṟāka vittuc collumiṭattu, ‘kapāṭam’ tāmpiraparaṇi kaṭalōṭu caṅkamikku miṭattil iruppatākac colalappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. aruccuṉaṉ teṟkē tīrtta yāttiri ceytatic collumiṭattu avaṉ kaliṅka nāṭṭukku ūṭākac ceṉṟu kōtāvariyiyum kiḻakkut toṭar maliyilēyuḷḷa makēntirattiyuṅ kaṭantu kāvēriyip piṉpuṟamāka viṭṭuk kiḻakkuk kaṭalōrattilē yirunta maṇalūriyaṭintāṉeṉac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
māpāratattiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṇalūreṉpatu ‘maṇaṟpaṭṭiṉam’ eṉṉum poruḷtarum taṉit tamiḻppeyarākum. ‘kapāṭam’eṉa irāmāyaṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu ‘alivāy’eṉṉum tamiḻppeyariṉ camakkiruta moḻi peyarppākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. alivāy tuṟi mukam eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ. paḻantamiḻk kaṭavuḷākiya murukaṉ kōyilukku iruppiṭamākiya centilmali aṭiyōrattil irunta tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉattukku alimukappaṭṭiṉameṉap peyaruḷatu. aḵtu ippōtuḷḷa tiruccentūrukkup pakkattilēyiruntatu. paḻiya murukaṉkōyil kaṭaluḷ maṟintuḷḷateṉac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. kapāṭapuram allatu alivāy akkālattu ellāviṭaṅkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭṭa pāṇṭiya aracaruṭiya tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉameṉṟum, ataṉarukē aracaṉuṭiya māḷikiyum aracirukkiyumirunta oru ciṟu paṭṭiṉamiruntateṉṟum karuta iṭamuṇṭu. maṇalūrilirunta kaṭicip pāṇṭiyaṉ alivāyik kaṭal koṇṭataṟpiṉ taṉatu talinakari maturikku māṟṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. immāṟṟam pāratap pōrukkup piṉṉātal vēṇṭum. itaṟkup piṉ kūṭṭappaṭṭa mūṉṟāñ caṅkatti 49 pāṇṭiyarkaḷ naṭattiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. iccaṅkam iṭiyīṭiṉṟi 10 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka ukkirap peruvaḻuti kālam variyil naṭipeṟṟiruk kiṉṟatu. ivaṉuṭiya kālam ki.pi. 30kkum 50kkum iṭiyil eṉṟu muṉṉamē muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 50 caṅkattiṉ iṟutikkālamākum. (caṅka kālattip paṟṟiya kuṟippu-paṇṭitar cavarirāyaṉ - tamiḻar purātaṉak kaliñaṉ-pakuti2 eṇ.1)1.
13. akattiyar
akattiyarippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ purāṇaṅkaḷilum itikācaṅkaḷi lum maṟṟum nūlkaḷilum malintu kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. appaṭiyirunta pōtum avarik kuṟitta uṇmi varalāṟaṉittum ārāyntu kōvip paṭuttik kūṟuvatu ilakuvaṉṟu. avaruṭiya piṟappik kuṟittup palavaki yāṉa katikaḷuṇṭu. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉa iyaṟkikku māṟākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ avi nampappaṭattakkaṉavalla. teṉṉāṭṭu muṉivaruḷ oruvar pulattiyar. ivaruṭiya putalvarukku akattiyareṉṟu peyar. pulattiyar ilaṅki vēntaṉākiya irāvaṇaṉiṉ pāṭṭaṉ. akattiyar kalacattiṟ piṟantāreṉpatum, avar peruvirali ṉaḷaviṉa reṉpatum, kaṭalik kuṭittāreṉpatum, vintiyamaliyi aṭakkiṉāreṉpatum purāṇak katikaḷ. akkatikaḷ nērākap poruḷkoḷḷaviyalā. cuvāmi tirukkaliyāṇattukku iruṭikaḷum muṉivarum tēvarum mēruvil īṇṭiyapōtu vaṭakōṭu tāḻntu teṉkōṭu uyarnta katikaḷumap paṭiyē. purāṇakkatikaḷik koṇṭu oru vakiyāṉa muṭipum koḷḷu tal kūṭātu. viruttiraṉ maḻimukileṉṟum intiraṉ maḻipeytaṟ kiṭamāyuḷḷa viṇṇeṉṟum tattuvārttam pakarkiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu uṇmiyāyiṉ akattiyar nakuṭaṉic capittatu, kaṭalik kuṭittatu mutaliya katikaḷ kaṟpaṉiyāy māṟukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭiyē purāṇak kati kaḷukkut tattuvapporuḷ kūṟap pukumiṭattu, uṇmiyeṉa nampappaṭṭu varum pala katikaḷ kaṟpaṉiyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. akattiyar imaya maliyi aṭuttuḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum peyarntu tamiḻnāṭu pōntār eṉpataṟkup purāṇakkatikaḷum avaṟṟit taḻuvi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tolkāppiyap pāyiravuriyil eḻutiya katiyumaṉṟi vēṟu piramāṇaṅ kaḷ illi. tamiḻ āriyappōr uṇṭāṉa kālattu itip pōṉṟa katikaḷ muḷittal iyalpu. akattiyar mikap paḻiya kālantoṭṭē potiyiṉ maliyil vacittāreṉpataṟku akattiyaṉ eṉṉum viṇmīṉukkup ‘potiyiṉ muṉivaṉ” eṉṉum peyar paripāṭaliṟ kāṇap paṭutalē pōtiya cāṉṟu. “teṇṇīraruvik kāṉār maliyat taruntavaṉ” (yāpparuṅkalakkāriki) akattiyar kuṭamaliyiṟ ṟaṅkip potiyiṉ malikku vantamiyiṉ avar kuṭamuṉiyeṉappaṭṭār eṉṟum, piṉ ṉuḷḷār ipporuḷi maṟantu (kuṭamuṉi) kalacayōṉi eṉṉum peyar kaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉar eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
“irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu avaṉi āṇṭu varāmal vilakki” eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟum maturik kāñci uriyāl akattiyar irāvaṇaṉ kālattavareṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṉik kaṭic caṅkap pulavaruḷoruvarākiya maturik kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār tāmiyaṟṟiya maṇimēkalik kāppiyattil kāviripūmpaṭṭiṉattirunta kakantaṉeṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ, paracurāmaṉ taṉṉōṭu pōr kuṟittu varu talit turkkā tēviyāluṇarntu avaḷēvaṟpaṭi akattiya muṉivaric caraṇaṭintāṉeṉṟum, avaṉ vēṇṭik koḷḷa, akattiyar kāviriyip perukac ceytāreṉṟum kūṟumāṟṟāl akattiyar irāmaṉ kālattut teṉṉāṭṭil uṟinta ceyti pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
“ārāyntu pārkkuṅkāl akattiyar parampariyoṉṟu irunta teṉṟum apparampariyil vantōraṉivarum akattiyar eṉṉum peyariyē vittukkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatum, avaruḷ oruvarē akattiyam eṉṉum nūlic ceytavareṉpatum, cila urikaḷil akattiyam eṉṟu kāṇappaṭum nūl iyaṟṟiya akattiyaṉār tolkāppiyaṉārkkup pintiyava reṉpatum. . . . . . .” -kā.namaccivāya mutaliyār.
akattiyariṭattiṟ kalvi kaṟṟōr paṉṉiruvareṉṟum, avarkaḷ ovvoruvarum ovvoru paṭalamākap puṟapporuṭ paṉṉirupaṭala miyaṟṟiṉārkaḷeṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ tolkāppiyar veṭcippaṭala miyaṟṟiṉāreṉṟum, tolkāppiya uri puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli mutaliyavaṟṟāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āciriyar iḷampūraṇavaṭikaḷ paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ aṭaṅkiya veṭcip paṭalam tolkāppiyar ceytataṉ ṟeṉap pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ kāṭṭi maṟuppar. ākavē, iḷampūraṇa vaṭikaḷ kālattil paṉṉirupaṭalatti ākkiyōrkaḷik kuṟittuc cantēkam iruntateṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
15. tolkāppiyar
tolkāppiyar akattiyar māṇavar paṉṉiruvaruḷ oruvareṉpa. avar iyaṟpeyar yāteṉap pulappaṭavilli. camatakkiṉi muṉivariṉ putalvar tolkāppiyareṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvar. camatakkiṉiyip paṟṟi eḻunta purāṇappakutikaḷil aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭāmiyāṉ, akkūṟṟu valiyuṭiyatākātu. tolkāppiyar akattiyattip piṉpaṟṟi nūl ceytāreṉpatu paramparik kati. avar tamatu nūlakattut tām akattiyattip piṉpaṟṟi nūl ceytāreṉṟāvatu akattiyar tam āciriya reṉṟāvatu kuṟippiṭṭilar. tolkāppiyattukkuc ciṟappuppāyirañ ceyta paṉampāraṉārum iv varalāṟu oṉṟaṉiyēṉum kūṟiṟṟilar. ivi ārāyccikkuriyaṉa.
1. tolkāppiyam
